 ## 4. 三条红线（课程评分点）
 
 1. **不编造**：driver 失败或未完成时，原样返回错误信封，绝不用占位数字冒充结果。
 2. **隔离 ≠ 修复**：`explain_result` 每次都同屏输出「数据量变化 + 隔离/去重/修复数 + 局限性」，
-   并在结尾固定附一句口径提醒。
+   并在结尾固定附一句提醒。
 3. **权威结果以 start + result 为准**：`quick_clean_demo` 只用于现场演示与快速取数。
 
 ## 5. 已实测（2026-09-25，Windows 本地 + 真实 ml-1m）
 
 用课程提供的注脏数据集跑 `--exec local --foreground`：
 
 | 项 | 实测值 |
 |---|---|
 | 输入 | ratings 1,150,241 / users 6,946 / movies 4,465 |
 | 输出 | ratings 1,000,209 / users 6,040 / movies 3,883 |

 ## 6. 已发现的文档/代码小差异（待与队长确认）
 
 | 项 | 接口文档 | 代码实测 |
 |---|---|---|
 | 阶段总数 | 序列列出 10 个 | `stage_total` 报 9 |
 | `report` 默认格式 | 示例写 `--format md` | 代码默认 `json` |

+两处都不影响联调（本 Agent 都显式传参），但汇报前建议统一说法。
 
 ## 7. 大模型层
 
 > 重构方向：**LLM 主控为默认路径**；模型不可用时**直接结构化报错**（不回退规则）。
 > 旧的「规则主路径 + 大模型可选兜底」与旧的措辞润色层已删除（计划 T2.5）。
 
 `/api/chat`（自然语言入口）只走 runtime 的 LLM 主控循环：模型选工具并填参数 → 执行 →
 结果回灌 → 基于真实结果组织回答。**模型不产出任何业务数字**——数字 100% 来自
 工具返回结果（`number_guard` 数字溯源护栏会拦截无法溯源的数字）。

 def _error_envelope(code, message, **detail):
     """接口文档 §3.10 的错误信封（HTTP 状态码一律 200，判据是 ok）。"""
     err = {"code": code, "message": message}
     for key, value in detail.items():
         if value is not None:
             err[key] = value
     return {"ok": False, "error": err}

     def _resource_call(self, func, *args):

+    def _relay_download(self, opener):
+        """带口令从远端取回并**流式**转给浏览器（不把整份文件读进内存）。
+
+        浏览器不持有口令：命中 Agent 的下载 URL，由 Agent 用运行时客户端的配置
+        （`Runtime.mcp_client.config`）带 `Authorization: Bearer <口令>` 去远端
+        取流，再原样转发。未接入 / 未配置（如 `NullMcpClient`）如实回
+        `MCP_UNAVAILABLE`；远端结构化错误按同一套映射转成 HTTP 状态 + 错误信封。
+        """
+        from . import mcp_download
+
+        runtime, err = self._runtime_or_error()
+        config = None
+        if err is None:
+            client = getattr(runtime, "mcp_client", None)
+            config = getattr(client, "config", None)
+        iterator, headers, error = opener(config)
+        if error is not None:
+            return self._send(mcp_download.status_for_code(error.get("code")),
+                              {"ok": False, "error": error})
+        return self._stream_response(iterator, headers)
+
+    def _stream_response(self, iterator, headers):
+        """写状态行 + 远端透传的下载头，然后分块转发响应体。"""
+        disposition = headers.get("Content-Disposition")
+        length = headers.get("Content-Length")
+        self.send_response(200)
+        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/octet-stream")
+        if disposition:
+            self.send_header("Content-Disposition", disposition)
+        if length:
+            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(length))
+        else:
+            # 远端没给长度：用关连接界定消息体（HTTP/1.1 合法），不臆造 Content-Length。
+            self.send_header("Connection", "close")
+            self.close_connection = True
+        self._cors()
+        self.end_headers()
+        try:
+            import httpx
+        except Exception:                            # pragma: no cover —— 缺依赖时降级
+            httpx = None
+        errors = (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError, ConnectionAbortedError,
+                  OSError, ValueError)
+        if httpx is not None:
+            # 远端转发中途断开（httpx.ReadError/HTTPError）不该冒到处理函数外：
+            # 头与部分体已发出，无法再回错误，按断流收尾即可。
+            errors = errors + (httpx.HTTPError,)
+        try:
+            for chunk in iterator:
+                self.wfile.write(chunk)
+            self._flush()
+        except errors:
+            pass                                    # 浏览器断开 / 远端断流：正常收尾
+        finally:
+            close = getattr(iterator, "close", None)
+            if callable(close):
+                try:
+                    close()                          # 触发远端响应/客户端关闭
+                except Exception:                    # pragma: no cover
+                    pass
+

+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""远端 Hadoop 能力服务的连接配置（标准协议入口 /mcp + Bearer 口令）。
+
+配置来源（后者覆盖前者）：
+  1) config/mcp.settings.json（可选，不建议入库）
+  2) 环境变量 AGENT_MCP_*
+
+红线：口令不落日志；默认不可用（地址或口令缺一即视为未接入）。
+"""
+
+import json
+import os
+
+from .mcp_contract import MCP_INTERFACE_VERSION
+
+_HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
+CONFIG_PATH = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(_HERE), "config", "mcp.settings.json")
+
+ENV = {
+    "url": "AGENT_MCP_URL",
+    "token": "AGENT_MCP_TOKEN",
+    "timeout_sec": "AGENT_MCP_TIMEOUT",
+    "interface_version": "AGENT_MCP_INTERFACE_VERSION",
+}
+
+DEFAULT_TIMEOUT = 15.0
+MAX_TIMEOUT = 120.0
+
+
+def _as_float(v, default):
+    try:
+        return float(v)
+    except (TypeError, ValueError):
+        return default
+
+
+class McpConfig(object):
+    def __init__(self, raw=None):
+        d = dict(raw or {})
+        self.url = (d.get("url") or "").strip()
+        self.token = (d.get("token") or "").strip()
+        self.timeout_sec = min(_as_float(d.get("timeout_sec"), DEFAULT_TIMEOUT), MAX_TIMEOUT)
+        self.interface_version = (d.get("interface_version") or MCP_INTERFACE_VERSION).strip()
+
+    @property
+    def usable(self):
+        return bool(self.url and self.token)
+
+    def _base(self):
+        base = self.url
+        for suffix in ("/mcp", "/"):
+            if base.endswith(suffix):
+                base = base[: -len(suffix)]
+        return base
+
+    def protocol_url(self):
+        return self._base() + "/mcp"
+
+    def health_url(self):
+        return self._base() + "/health"
+
+    def safe_dict(self):
+        """脱敏视图：不含口令。"""
+        return {"url": self.url or None, "token_set": bool(self.token),
+                "timeout_sec": self.timeout_sec,
+                "interface_version": self.interface_version, "usable": self.usable}
+
+
+def _read_file():
+    if not os.path.isfile(CONFIG_PATH):
+        return {}
+    try:
+        with open(CONFIG_PATH, "r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
+            d = json.load(fh)
+        return d if isinstance(d, dict) else {}
+    except (ValueError, OSError):
+        return {}
+
+
+def load(overrides=None):
+    raw = {}
+    raw.update(_read_file())
+    for key, name in ENV.items():
+        val = os.environ.get(name)
+        if val not in (None, ""):
+            raw[key] = val
+    if isinstance(overrides, dict):
+        raw.update(overrides)
+    return McpConfig(raw)

 STRUCTURED_ERROR_CODES = (

+#: 拒绝/排除原因的**细类**（写进错误细节的 `reason_kind` 字段）—— 与远端读取层

+REASON_KINDS = ("control", "sidecar", "residue", "directory", "not_regular_file")

+#: driver 自产码的中文说明（不在 §5.4 表内，但与结构化码一并给出可读文案）。
+_DRIVER_NATIVE_MESSAGES = {

+def describe_error(error):
+    """把一个错误（dict 或码字符串）翻成面向用户的**可读中文**。
+
+    回退顺序（前者命中即返回）：
+      1. 已知结构化码 / driver 自产码 → 本端固定中文说明；
+      2. 远端信封里的 `error.message`（原样转述，不改成别的结论）；
+      3. 原始码（未知码也不丢，便于排障）；
+      4. 兜底说明。
+    """
+    code = None
+    message = None
+    if isinstance(error, dict):
+        code = error.get("code")
+        message = error.get("message")
+    elif isinstance(error, str):
+        code = error
+    elif error is not None:
+        message = str(error)
+
+    if code in ERROR_MESSAGES:
+        return ERROR_MESSAGES[code]
+    if code in _DRIVER_NATIVE_MESSAGES:
+        return _DRIVER_NATIVE_MESSAGES[code]
+    if message:
+        return str(message)
+    if code:
+        return str(code)
+    return UNKNOWN_ERROR_MESSAGE
+
+
 def normalize_result(envelope):

     """
     if not isinstance(envelope, dict):
         return False, {"ok": False, "error": {
             "code": CODE_DRIVER_BAD_OUTPUT,
             "message": "后端返回非信封：%r" % (envelope,)}}
     if envelope.get("ok"):

+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""远端两段只读下载路由的带口令流式取回。
+
+远端 Hadoop 能力服务以普通 HTTP GET 暴露两条只读路由（都要口令）：
+    /download/task/{task_id}/{path}
+    /download/published/{data_version}/{table}
+
+浏览器不能持有口令，因此由 Agent 带 `Authorization: Bearer <口令>` 取回，再把响应体
+**流式**转给浏览器（一次只在内存里放一块，绝不整份读进内存）。
+
+非 2xx 时读远端信封，**原样保留**它的 `error.code` / `error.message` 与其余细节
+（如 `reason_kind`），不把结构化错误改写成泛化错误。
+
+红线：口令只放进请求头，**绝不**写日志 / 信封 / 任何用户可见输出。
+"""
+
+from urllib.parse import quote
+
+from .mcp_http_client import explain
+
+#: 下载失败码 → 给浏览器看的 HTTP 状态（与远端 `hadoop/mcpserver/server.py` 的下载
+#: 映射同一套；`MCP_UNAVAILABLE` 是 Agent 侧新增的"未接入"，按不可达处理）。
+STATUS_BY_CODE = {
+    "UNAUTHORIZED": 401,
+    "FORBIDDEN_HOST": 403,
+    "FORBIDDEN_PATH": 400,
+    "USAGE": 400,
+    "FILE_NOT_FOUND": 404,
+    "PRODUCT_INVALID": 404,
+    "TASK_NOT_FOUND": 404,
+    "MCP_UNAVAILABLE": 503,
+    "DRIVER_UNREACHABLE": 503,
+    "DRIVER_TIMEOUT": 504,
+}
+
+#: 远端只回了 HTTP 状态、没给结构化信封时的兜底码（保留一层可读语义）。
+_CODE_BY_STATUS = {
+    400: "USAGE",
+    401: "UNAUTHORIZED",
+    403: "FORBIDDEN_HOST",
+    404: "FILE_NOT_FOUND",
+    503: "DRIVER_UNREACHABLE",
+    504: "DRIVER_TIMEOUT",
+}
+
+#: 既无信封又无已知状态时的兜底码。
+DEFAULT_ERROR_CODE = "DRIVER_BAD_OUTPUT"
+
+#: 本机越界时回的结构化错误（与远端 `forbidden_path` 同一契约；不发起请求）。
+FORBIDDEN_PATH_ERROR = {"code": "FORBIDDEN_PATH", "message": "该路径不允许访问"}
+
+
+def _has_forbidden_form(value):
+    """值里有没有「本机必须先拒」的形态：NUL / 反斜杠 / 绝对路径。"""
+    s = "" if value is None else str(value)
+    return "\x00" in s or "\\" in s or s.startswith("/")
+
+
+def _safe_relpath(relpath):
+    """任务产物相对路径：允许多段 `a/b/c`，拒绝对路径 / 反斜杠 / NUL / `..` 段。
+
+    httpx 发送前会按 RFC 3986 归一化 URL 的点段；若放行 `..`，
+    `/download/task/{id}/../../etc/passwd` 会被折叠掉路由段（越界被静默改写），
+    因此先把 `..` 段挡在本机。隐藏文件/控制文件的判断仍留给服务端（第二道防线）。
+    """
+    if _has_forbidden_form(relpath):
+        return False
+    text = "" if relpath is None else str(relpath)
+    return ".." not in text.split("/")
+
+
+def _safe_segment(value):
+    """已发布下载的单一路径分段：不得含 `/`，也不得是 `.` / `..` / 绝对 / 反斜杠。"""
+    if _has_forbidden_form(value):
+        return False
+    text = "" if value is None else str(value)
+    return "/" not in text and text not in (".", "..")
+
+
+
+def status_for_code(code, default=502):
+    """结构化错误码 → 给浏览器的 HTTP 状态（未知码按 502，如实暴露异常）。"""
+    return STATUS_BY_CODE.get(code, default)
+
+
+def base_url(config):
+    """从配置里取服务根地址（去掉协议入口后缀 `/mcp`）。"""
+    url = (getattr(config, "url", "") or "").strip()
+    for suffix in ("/mcp", "/"):
+        if url.endswith(suffix):
+            url = url[: -len(suffix)]
+    return url
+
+
+def _error_from_response(response):
+    """非 2xx：读信封 JSON，**保留**服务端的 `error.code` / `message` 与细节。
+
+    读不到合法信封时按 HTTP 状态给兜底码，不臆造成功、不吞掉失败。
+    """
+    payload = None
+    try:
+        payload = response.json()
+    except Exception:                                # noqa: BLE001 —— 非 JSON 就是没有信封
+        payload = None
+    if isinstance(payload, dict):
+        error = payload.get("error")
+        if isinstance(error, dict):
+            out = {
+                "code": error.get("code") or _CODE_BY_STATUS.get(
+                    response.status_code, DEFAULT_ERROR_CODE),
+                "message": error.get("message") or "远端下载失败",
+            }
+            for key, value in error.items():
+                if key not in ("code", "message"):
+                    out[key] = value             # reason_kind / path 等细节原样带出
+            return out
+    return {"code": _CODE_BY_STATUS.get(response.status_code, DEFAULT_ERROR_CODE),
+            "message": "远端下载失败（HTTP %s）" % response.status_code}
+
+
+def _close_quietly(obj):
+    try:
+        obj.close()
+    except Exception:                            # noqa: BLE001 —— 收尾不掩盖主流程
+        pass
+
+
+def _iter_stream(response, client):
+    """逐块吐出响应体；无论正常结束还是对端断开都关掉响应与客户端。"""
+    try:
+        for chunk in response.iter_bytes():
+            if chunk:
+                yield chunk
+    finally:
+        _close_quietly(response)
+        _close_quietly(client)
+
+
+def _open(config, url, transport=None):
+    """发起一次流式 GET，返回 `(iterator, headers, error)`。三选一非空。"""
+    import httpx
+
+    if config is None or not getattr(config, "usable", False):
+        return None, None, {"code": "MCP_UNAVAILABLE",
+                            "message": "远端能力服务未接入或未配置：下载"}
+
+    if transport is None:
+        transport = getattr(config, "transport", None)   # 仅测试注入用接缝
+
+    headers = {"Authorization": "Bearer %s" % config.token}
+    client = httpx.Client(transport=transport,
+                          timeout=getattr(config, "timeout_sec", 15.0))
+    try:
+        request = client.build_request("GET", url, headers=headers)
+        response = client.send(request, stream=True)
+    except Exception as exc:                         # noqa: BLE001 —— 这里就是要报清楚
+        _close_quietly(client)
+        return None, None, {"code": "DRIVER_UNREACHABLE",
+                            "message": explain(exc, url)}
+
+    if not (200 <= response.status_code < 300):
+        error = _error_from_response(response)
+        _close_quietly(response)
+        _close_quietly(client)
+        return None, None, error
+
+    out_headers = {}
+    disposition = response.headers.get("content-disposition")
+    if disposition:
+        out_headers["Content-Disposition"] = disposition
+    length = response.headers.get("content-length")
+    if length:
+        out_headers["Content-Length"] = length
+    return _iter_stream(response, client), out_headers, None
+
+
+def open_task_stream(config, hadoop_task_id, relpath, transport=None):
+    """任务产物下载（`/download/task/{task_id}/{path}`）；返回 `(iterator, headers, error)`。"""
+    if not _safe_relpath(relpath):
+        return None, None, dict(FORBIDDEN_PATH_ERROR)   # 本机先拒：不发请求
+    url = "%s/download/task/%s/%s" % (
+        base_url(config), quote(str(hadoop_task_id or ""), safe=""),
+        quote(str(relpath or ""), safe="/"))
+    return _open(config, url, transport)
+
+
+def open_published_stream(config, data_version, table, transport=None):
+    """已发布数据下载（`/download/published/{data_version}/{table}`）。"""
+    if not (_safe_segment(data_version) and _safe_segment(table)):
+        return None, None, dict(FORBIDDEN_PATH_ERROR)   # 本机先拒：不发请求
+    url = "%s/download/published/%s/%s" % (
+        base_url(config), quote(str(data_version or ""), safe=""),
+        quote(str(table or ""), safe=""))
+    return _open(config, url, transport)

new file mode 100644

+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""真实 MCP 客户端：按标准协议连远端能力服务的 /mcp 入口。
+
+连接方式与 `hadoop/tools/mcp_client.py` 一致：带鉴权头的 HTTP 客户端 + 传输层 + 会话，
+每次动作开一次短会话，动作完成即关。任何连接/协议异常都翻成可读说明，不把堆栈甩给上层。
+
+红线：口令只放进请求头 `Authorization: Bearer <口令>`，**绝不**进日志或信封。
+不可用（无地址或无口令）时所有调用返回结构化 `MCP_UNAVAILABLE`，不发起任何网络请求。
+"""
+
+import asyncio
+import json
+
+from . import mcp_contract
+from .mcp_config import McpConfig
+
+#: 远端工具在工具表里的来源标记（契约层约定，全局唯一）。
+SOURCE_MCP = "mcp"
+
+
+def _flatten(exc):
+    """把异常组摊平：连接失败常被包成异常组，真正的原因藏在里层。"""
+    out = []
+    stack = [exc]
+    seen = set()
+    while stack:
+        item = stack.pop()
+        if id(item) in seen:
+            continue
+        seen.add(id(item))
+        out.append(item)
+        for sub in getattr(item, "exceptions", ()) or ():
+            stack.append(sub)
+    return out
+
+
+def explain(exc, url):
+    """把底层异常翻成能照着做的话（区分：口令不对 / 连不上 / 其它）。"""
+    flat = _flatten(exc)
+    text = "；".join("%s: %s" % (type(x).__name__, x) for x in flat[:3]) or str(exc)
+    low = " ".join(str(x).lower() for x in flat) or str(exc).lower()
+    if "401" in low or "unauthorized" in low:
+        return ("连接凭据不对：确认口令与请求头 Authorization: Bearer <口令>。"
+                "原始错误：%s" % text)
+    if "refused" in low or "connect" in low or "unreachable" in low:
+        return ("连不上 %s：服务可能没启动，或宿主机侧没配端口转发（虚拟机侧固定 8765）。"
+                "原始错误：%s" % (url, text))
+    return "调用失败：%s" % text
+
+
+class McpHttpClient(mcp_contract.McpClient):
+    """标准协议 MCP 客户端；`session_factory` 仅供测试注入（不连网）。"""
+
+    def __init__(self, config=None, session_factory=None):
+        self._config = config or McpConfig({})
+        self._session_factory = session_factory
+        self.interface_version = self._config.interface_version
+
+    @property
+    def config(self):
+        return self._config
+
+    # -- 内部 -----------------------------------------------------------------
+
+    def _envelope_error(self, code, message):
+        return {"ok": False, "interface_version": self.interface_version,
+                "error": {"code": code, "message": message}}
+
+    def _unavailable(self, action):
+        return self._envelope_error(
+            mcp_contract.CODE_MCP_UNAVAILABLE,
+            "远端能力服务未接入或未配置：%s" % action)
+
+    def _run(self, worker):
+        """开一次会话跑 `await worker(session)`；异常翻成 `{"_error": <可读说明>}`。
+
+        `worker` 是一个 `async def`，接收已握手的会话。
+        """
+        if self._session_factory is not None:
+            async def _fake():
+                session = self._session_factory()
+                await session.initialize()
+                return await worker(session)
+
+            try:
+                return asyncio.run(_fake())
+            except Exception as exc:                    # noqa: BLE001 —— 这里就是要报清楚
+                return {"_error": explain(exc, self._config.protocol_url())}
+
+        from mcp import ClientSession
+        from mcp.client.streamable_http import (
+            create_mcp_http_client, streamable_http_client)
+
+        headers = {"Authorization": "Bearer %s" % self._config.token}
+
+        async def _real():
+            async with create_mcp_http_client(headers=headers) as http_client:
+                async with streamable_http_client(
+                        self._config.protocol_url(), http_client=http_client) as streams:
+                    async with ClientSession(streams[0], streams[1]) as session:
+                        await session.initialize()
+                        return await worker(session)
+
+        try:
+            return asyncio.run(_real())
+        except Exception as exc:                        # noqa: BLE001 —— 这里就是要报清楚
+            return {"_error": explain(exc, self._config.protocol_url())}
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _tool_row(tool):
+        schema = getattr(tool, "input_schema", None) or {}
+        return {"name": tool.name,
+                "title": getattr(tool, "title", None) or tool.name,
+                "description": getattr(tool, "description", "") or "",
+                "source": SOURCE_MCP,
+                "parameters": schema}
+
+    # -- 契约方法 -------------------------------------------------------------
+
+    def list_tools(self):
+        if not self._config.usable:
+            return []
+
+        async def worker(session):
+            result = await session.list_tools()
+            return [self._tool_row(tool)
+                    for tool in list(getattr(result, "tools", []) or [])]
+
+        rows = self._run(worker)
+        return rows if isinstance(rows, list) else []
+
+    def call_tool(self, name, args):
+        if not self._config.usable:
+            return self._unavailable("call_tool(%s)" % name)
+
+        async def worker(session):
+            result = await session.call_tool(name, args or {})
+            texts = []
+            for item in getattr(result, "content", []) or []:
+                text = getattr(item, "text", None)
+                if text is not None:
+                    texts.append(text)
+            return "\n".join(texts)
+
+        text = self._run(worker)
+        if isinstance(text, dict) and text.get("_error"):
+            return self._envelope_error(mcp_contract.CODE_DRIVER_UNREACHABLE,
+                                        text["_error"])
+        try:
+            payload = json.loads(text)
+        except (ValueError, TypeError):
+            return self._envelope_error(
+                mcp_contract.CODE_DRIVER_BAD_OUTPUT,
+                "远端返回非信封：%s" % str(text)[:300])
+        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
+            return self._envelope_error(mcp_contract.CODE_DRIVER_BAD_OUTPUT,
+                                        "远端返回非对象信封")
+        return payload
+
+    def get_task_status(self, task_id):
+        return self.call_tool("get_task_status", {"task_id": task_id})
+
+    def describe_capabilities(self):
+        return self.call_tool("describe_capabilities", {})
+
+    # -- 启动时一致性校验 -----------------------------------------------------
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _declined(reason):
+        """校验没跑成（未接入 / 调用失败）时的**非抛**结果，带可读原因。"""
+        return {"ok": False, "missing": [], "extra": [],
+                "version_ok": False, "peer_version": None, "reason": reason}
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _tool_names_in(envelope):
+        """从能力自描述信封里取工具名集合（`tools` 为条目 dict 或裸名串）。"""
+        names = set()
+        for item in envelope.get("tools") or []:
+            name = item.get("name") if isinstance(item, dict) else item
+            if isinstance(name, str) and name:
+                names.add(name)
+        return names
+
+    def verify_tools(self, declared):
+        """用能力自描述校验本端工具表与远端是否一致（**绝不抛异常**）。
+
+        返回 `{"ok", "missing", "extra", "version_ok", "peer_version"}`：
+          * `missing` —— 本端声明而远端没有的工具名（排序）；
+          * `extra`   —— 远端有而本端未声明的工具名（排序）；
+          * `ok`      —— 名字集合完全一致且接口版本同主版本时为 True。
+
+        客户端不可用 / 调用失败时返回 `ok=False` 且带 `reason`，不改写、不静默。
+        """
+        declared = set(declared or ())
+        if not self._config.usable:
+            return self._declined("远端能力服务未接入或未配置：verify_tools")
+        envelope = self.describe_capabilities()
+        if not isinstance(envelope, dict) or not envelope.get("ok"):
+            error = (envelope or {}).get("error") or {}
+            return self._declined(error.get("message") or "能力自描述调用失败")
+        peer_version = envelope.get("interface_version")
+        server_names = self._tool_names_in(envelope)
+        missing = sorted(declared - server_names)
+        extra = sorted(server_names - declared)
+        version_ok = bool(mcp_contract.negotiate(
+            peer_version, self.interface_version).get("compatible"))
+        return {"ok": not missing and not extra and version_ok,
+                "missing": missing, "extra": extra,
+                "version_ok": version_ok, "peer_version": peer_version}
+
+    def health(self):
+        """健康检查：不进协议入口，直接 GET /health（免口令）。"""
+        if not self._config.usable:
+            return self._unavailable("health")
+        import httpx
+        try:
+            resp = httpx.get(self._config.health_url(),
+                             timeout=self._config.timeout_sec)
+            data = resp.json()
+            return data if isinstance(data, dict) else {"ok": True, "raw": data}
+        except Exception as exc:                        # noqa: BLE001 —— 这里就是要报清楚
+            return self._envelope_error(
+                mcp_contract.CODE_DRIVER_UNREACHABLE,
+                explain(exc, self._config.health_url()))

+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""远端能力服务的工具表（source=mcp）—— Agent 侧工具形状的权威来源。
+
+参数名 / 默认值 / 枚举 / 取值范围以远端能力自描述为准
+（`hadoop/mcpserver/tools.py` 的 `TOOLS`）。本表是「对端」（Agent）据以刷新
+自己工具表的权威形状；运行时可用能力自描述校验一致性。
+
+只有「快速演示清洗」(`quick_clean_demo`) 留在本机，见 `LOCAL_ONLY_TOOLS`。
+"""
+
+SOURCE_MCP = "mcp"
+
+#: 参数类型（与远端 `hadoop/mcpserver/tools.py` 的取值对齐）。
+PARAM_STRING = "string"
+PARAM_INT = "int"
+PARAM_BOOL = "bool"
+PARAM_ENUM = "enum"
+
+#: 远端参数类型 → JSON Schema 类型。
+_JSON_TYPE = {
+    PARAM_STRING: "string",
+    PARAM_INT: "integer",
+    PARAM_BOOL: "boolean",
+    PARAM_ENUM: "string",
+}
+
+
+def _param(name, type_, description, required=False, default=None,
+           values=None, minimum=None, maximum=None):
+    """一条远端参数说明（照 apparat 侧 `_p` 的形状，供 `_schema` 转 JSON Schema）。"""
+    return {
+        "name": name,
+        "type": type_,
+        "description": description,
+        "required": bool(required),
+        "default": default,
+        "values": tuple(values) if values is not None else None,
+        "minimum": minimum,
+        "maximum": maximum,
+    }
+
+
+def _schema(params):
+    """把远端参数列表转成 JSON Schema 的 object 形态（properties + required）。"""
+    props = {}
+    required = []
+    for param in params:
+        entry = {"type": _JSON_TYPE.get(param["type"], param["type"])}
+        if param["description"]:
+            entry["description"] = param["description"]
+        if param["values"] is not None:
+            entry["enum"] = list(param["values"])
+        if param["default"] is not None:
+            entry["default"] = param["default"]
+        if param["minimum"] is not None:
+            entry["minimum"] = param["minimum"]
+        if param["maximum"] is not None:
+            entry["maximum"] = param["maximum"]
+        props[param["name"]] = entry
+        if param["required"]:
+            required.append(param["name"])
+    return {"type": "object", "properties": props, "required": required}
+
+
+def _tool(name, description, params, long_running, estimated_seconds):
+    return {
+        "name": name,
+        "source": SOURCE_MCP,
+        "long_running": long_running,
+        "estimated_seconds": estimated_seconds,
+        "description": description,
+        "parameters": _schema(params),
+    }
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# 复用参数说明（与远端同名同默认值）
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+TASK_ID = _param("task_id", PARAM_STRING, "任务编号。", required=True)
+
+SCOPE = _param(
+    "scope", PARAM_ENUM,
+    "数据范围。全部：完整数据，正式用途；小样本：仅联调，且不会进入发布区。",
+    default="full", values=("full", "sample"))
+
+RULES = _param(
+    "rules", PARAM_STRING,
+    "清洗方案文件路径。缺省用已登记的默认方案；自定义方案请先校验再执行。")
+
+SCORING = _param(
+    "scoring", PARAM_STRING,
+    "评分方案文件路径。缺省用已登记的默认方案；自定义方案请先校验再执行。")
+
+DATA_VERSION = _param(
+    "data_version", PARAM_STRING,
+    "数据版本号。缺省取方案里声明的版本；与方案声明不一致会被拒绝。")
+
+TAG = _param("tag", PARAM_STRING, "任务标签，便于人辨认，不影响执行结果。")
+
+FORCE = _param(
+    "force", PARAM_BOOL,
+    "忽略「已有任务在运行」的冲突。默认关闭；开启后返回里会提示风险。",
+    default=False)
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# 22 项远端能力（逐项照 `hadoop/mcpserver/tools.py` 转写）
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+REMOTE_TOOL_SPECS = {

 class ResourceError(Exception):
     """结构化错误（对应 §3.10 错误信封）。http_api 直接 `envelope()` 序列化。"""
 
     def __init__(self, code, message, **detail):
         Exception.__init__(self, message)
         self.code = code
         self.message = message
         self.detail = {k: v for k, v in detail.items() if v is not None}
 
     def envelope(self):
         err = {"code": self.code, "message": self.message}
         err.update(self.detail)
         return {"ok": False, "error": err}
 
 
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# 远端调用接缝：资源类端点改由远端能力服务提供，本层只做字段映射
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+def _remote(client, name, args=None):
+    """调远端能力并返回**成功**信封；失败如实抛 `ResourceError`。
+
+    约定（与 `agent/mcp_contract.py` 的一致）：
+      * `client is None`（本机没有接入远端）→ `MCP_UNAVAILABLE`，不假装成功；
+      * 远端 `ok=false` → 原样转述它的 `error.code` / `error.message` 与其余细节
+        （如 `reason_kind`），不换成别的结论、不填占位值；
+      * 远端返回不是对象信封 → `DRIVER_BAD_OUTPUT`。
+
+    `ResourceError.envelope()` 会把细节并进 `error` 对象，出口形状与前端既有的
+    `{"ok": false, "error": {code, message, …}}` 完全一致。
+    """
+    if client is None:
+        raise ResourceError("MCP_UNAVAILABLE",
+                            "远端能力服务未接入或未配置：%s" % name)
+    env = client.call_tool(name, args or {})
+    if not isinstance(env, dict):
+        raise ResourceError("DRIVER_BAD_OUTPUT", "远端返回非信封：%r" % (env,))
+    if not env.get("ok"):
+        err = env.get("error")
+        err = err if isinstance(err, dict) else {}
+        code = err.get("code") or "DRIVER_BAD_OUTPUT"
+        message = err.get("message") or ("远端调用失败：%s" % name)
+        detail = {k: v for k, v in err.items() if k not in ("code", "message")}
+        raise ResourceError(code, message, **detail)
+    return env
+
+

 def _file_parts(parts, field):
     """取上传文件段：优先按字段名，退化取任意带 filename 的段（兼容前端差异）。"""
     chosen = [p for p in parts if p.get("name") == field and p.get("filename")]
     if not chosen:
         chosen = [p for p in parts if p.get("filename")]
     return chosen

+    """列已登记方案（远端 `list_schemes`）。

+    前端接受 `schemes` / `items` / `list` / `data` 任一种键，因此这里保持宽松：

+    """列本机任务目录下的全部文件（`path` / `size` / `kind`）。

+class RemoteTaskProbe(object):
+    """远端探针：用 MCP 客户端的 `get_task_status`（必要时补 `get_task_result`）查状态。
+
+    与 `DriverTaskProbe.probe` 同形状（`probe(record) -> dict | None`），故可在
+    `LongTaskManager.poll(probe)` / 后台轮询里无缝替换。只做**只读查询**：
+      * 用 `hadoop_task_id` 调远端 `get_task_status`（双编号纪律：远端一律用 hadoop 编号）；
+      * 成功任务再调 `get_task_result(hadoop_task_id)` 取权威结果，供完成路径走
+        `result_payload`（失败/未完成时 result 为 None，如实反映）；
+      * 缺 `hadoop_task_id` / 传输异常 / 任务不存在（信封 `ok=false`）一律返回 None，绝不抛。
+    """
+
+    def __init__(self, client):
+        self._client = client
+
+    def probe(self, record):
+        hadoop_id = (record or {}).get("hadoop_task_id")
+        if not hadoop_id:
+            return None
+        try:
+            envelope = self._client.call_tool(
+                "get_task_status", {"task_id": hadoop_id})
+        except Exception:
+            return None
+        if not isinstance(envelope, dict) or not envelope.get("ok"):
+            return None
+        seen = {
+            "status": envelope.get("status"),
+            "stage": envelope.get("stage"),
+            "progress_percent": envelope.get("progress_percent"),
+            "stage_index": envelope.get("stage_index"),
+            "stage_total": envelope.get("stage_total"),
+            "message": envelope.get("message"),
+            "errors": envelope.get("errors"),
+        }
+        if seen["status"] == SUCCEEDED:
+            seen["result"] = self._result(hadoop_id)
+        return seen
+
+    def _result(self, hadoop_id):
+        """成功任务取权威结果信封；拿不到就返回 None（不臆造）。"""
+        try:
+            envelope = self._client.call_tool(
+                "get_task_result", {"task_id": hadoop_id})
+        except Exception:
+            return None
+        if isinstance(envelope, dict) and envelope.get("ok"):
+            return envelope
+        return None
+
+

 为什么自建表、不只靠 LangGraph 检查点（阶段 0 结论 §6.4）：
   检查点是「每条 thread 一条线性执行日志」，而对话要成**树**（阶段 7 分支/跳回）。
   因此这里另立权威表：
     conversations  对话（编号、标题、时间、当前所在节点、上下文）
     nodes          回合节点（编号、父节点、角色、时间、摘要、内容）
   检查点只当**执行日志 / LLM 上下文消息**用；血缘以本表为准。
 
 数据模型
 --------
 对话 conversation：有编号（`c0001`…）、标题、创建/更新时间、当前所在节点、
-  上下文（当前任务编号、数据集版本、以及直调口径快照 opts）。
+  上下文（当前任务编号、数据集版本、以及直调参数快照 opts）。
 节点 node：有编号（自增整数）、**父节点**（parent_id，根为 None）、角色
   （user / assistant）、创建时间、可读摘要、内容（大内容走 content_ref 外置）。
 
 血缘：**有向树**（parent_id 串起来，一个节点可有多个子节点）。`ancestors()` 从任意
 节点回溯到根。阶段 7 起：
   * 一个节点可挂多条子链 —— 从某节点「继续」即在其下新开一支，旧后文原样保留；
   * `branches` 表登记每条分支（分支点节点、分支专属 thread_id、派生自哪条线程）；
   * 对话的 `current_branch` 记当前所在分支的 thread_id（NULL 即主支 = 对话编号）；
   * 节点上的 `thread_id` / `checkpoint_id` 是「该节点所处执行线程 + 其检查点」的回溯线索。

 def _signature_defaults(fn):
     """从工具实现的签名里取各参数的默认值（供 §3.7「参数与默认值」）。"""
     if fn is None:
         return {}
     try:
         sig = inspect.signature(fn)
     except (TypeError, ValueError):
         return {}
     out = {}
     for name, param in sig.parameters.items():
         if param.default is not inspect.Parameter.empty:
             out[name] = param.default
     return out

+    """把一个 JSON Schema 属性整理成 §3.7 要的「参数」条目。
+
+    默认值优先取 schema 里声明的（远端工具表的权威默认值），
+    其次回退到本机实现的签名默认值（本机专属工具）。
+    """
     entry = {"name": name, "type": prop.get("type", "string"),
              "required": name in required}
-    if name in defaults:
+    if "default" in prop:
+        entry["default"] = prop["default"]
+    elif name in defaults:
         entry["default"] = defaults[name]
     elif not entry["required"]:
         entry["default"] = None
     if "enum" in prop:
         entry["enum"] = list(prop["enum"])
     for key in ("minimum", "maximum"):
         if key in prop:
             entry[key] = prop[key]
     if prop.get("description"):
         entry["description"] = prop["description"]
@@ -128,22 +155,22 @@ def tool_table():

+import threading

+from .. import mcp_contract, mcp_tools, tool_specs

+
+class StartupToolCheck(object):
+    """启动时「本端工具表 ↔ 远端能力自描述」校验结果的线程安全持有者。
+
+    每个注入的客户端 / 后端各持一个，取代旧模块级单槽：多次 `build_tool_backend`
+    互不覆盖，读结果的人拿到的正是它实际使用的那份。读写都在同一把锁下，`/health`
+    可以在后台线程跑完前安全地读（读到 `None`）。
+    """
+
+    def __init__(self):
+        self._lock = threading.Lock()
+        self._result = None
+        self._thread = None
+
+    def run(self, worker):
+        """在后台 daemon 线程跑 `worker()`，结果写入本持有者（非阻塞装配）。"""
+        def _body():
+            self.record(worker())
+
+        thread = threading.Thread(target=_body, name="mcp-startup-tool-check",
+                                  daemon=True)
+        with self._lock:
+            self._thread = thread
+        thread.start()
+        return thread
+
+    def record(self, result):
+        with self._lock:
+            self._result = result
+
+    def result(self):
+        with self._lock:
+            return self._result
+
+    def wait(self, timeout=None):
+        with self._lock:
+            thread = self._thread
+        if thread is not None:
+            thread.join(timeout)
+        return self.result()

         raise NotImplementedError

 class LongTaskBackend(ToolBackend):
     """执行出口的长任务装饰器（阶段 4 T4.1）。
 
     在**任何**工具执行后端（本地 / 假 / 将来 MCP）的出口做同一件事：工具成功且
-    属长耗时时，登记一个长任务并把句柄并入信封，**当轮立即返回、不阻塞**。
-    装饰器只暴露 `execute(name, args)`，与循环/tool_specs 的调用形状一致，故上层
-    循环无需感知长任务；阶段 8 的 MCP 后端套上它即获得同样的挂起能力。
+    属**任务创建类**长耗时时，登记一个长任务并把句柄并入信封，**当轮立即返回、
+    不阻塞**。装饰器只暴露 `execute(name, args)`，与循环/tool_specs 的调用形状
+    一致，故上层循环无需感知长任务；阶段 8 的 MCP 后端套上它即获得同样的挂起能力。
+
+    判据是 `creates_task`（只有 start_cleaning_task / start_clean_task / score_task
+    这类「发起任务」的工具），**不是** `is_long_running`：`wait_for_task` 虽被标为
+    长耗时，但它只是阻塞等待既有任务，登记它会凭空多出一个长任务。
     """
 
     def __init__(self, inner, manager, conversation_id):
         self._inner = inner
         self._manager = manager
         self._conversation_id = conversation_id
 
     def execute(self, name, args):
         ok, envelope = self._inner.execute(name, args)
         if not ok or self._manager is None:
             return ok, envelope
-        if not self._manager.is_long_running(name):
+        if not self._manager.creates_task(name):
             return ok, envelope
         handle = self._manager.register(self._conversation_id, name, args, envelope)
         envelope = dict(envelope or {})
         envelope["long_task"] = True
         envelope["agent_task_id"] = handle["agent_task_id"]
         envelope["task_handle"] = handle
         return ok, envelope
 
     def __getattr__(self, item):
         return getattr(self._inner, item)

+class FakeRemoteClient(object):
+    """假远端客户端：按工具名回罐头信封，并记下每次调用的参数（不连网）。"""
+
+    def __init__(self, responses=None):
+        self.responses = dict(responses or {})
+        self.calls = []
+
+    def call_tool(self, name, args):
+        self.calls.append((name, dict(args or {})))
+        handler = self.responses.get(name)
+        if handler is None:
+            return {"ok": False, "error": {
+                "code": "MCP_UNAVAILABLE",
+                "message": "本测试没有为 %s 准备罐头信封" % name}}
+        return handler(args or {}) if callable(handler) else handler
+
+    def calls_for(self, name):
+        return [args for called, args in self.calls if called == name]
+
+

+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""端到端本地链路：经运行时工具后端 + 假 MCP 客户端走完整条长任务路径。
+
+全程**不连网**（不碰 127.0.0.1:8900）：用本文件里的 `FakeRemoteClient`（假 MCP
+客户端）顶替真实远端。覆盖一条完整链路：
+
+    发起清洗（长任务） → get_task_status（进度流动） → 终态 → get_task_result
+
+断言四件事：
+  * 长任务被登记（`agent_task_id` + `hadoop_task_id` 写进对话上下文）；
+  * 运行期进度经查状态流动（progress_percent / stage 同步进记录并推 task_status）；
+  * 到终态后取回权威结果并推 `result_payload`；
+  * 终态收集到产物（经远端 `get_task_result` 的 `artifacts[]`）并推 `artifact_added`。
+
+假客户端在 `FakeRemoteClient` 里显式声明（本测试的唯一外部依赖替身）。
+"""
+import os
+import sys
+import tempfile
+import unittest
+
+AGENT_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+REPO_ROOT = os.path.dirname(AGENT_DIR)
+sys.path.insert(0, REPO_ROOT)
+
+from agent import mcp_contract  # noqa: E402
+from agent.llm import ModelError, NOT_CONFIGURED  # noqa: E402
+from agent.runtime import (ARTIFACT_ADDED, MESSAGE_DONE, RESULT_PAYLOAD,  # noqa: E402
+                           TASK_STATUS, RemoteTaskProbe, Runtime)
+

+class FakeRemoteClient(mcp_contract.McpClient):
+    """假 MCP 客户端（无网络）：受控返回「发起 / 查状态 / 取结果」三类信封。
+
+    状态可由测试直接改写（`status` / `progress` / `stage`），从而驱动
+    「运行中 → 终态」的时序；每次调用都记录在 `calls` 里，供断言双编号与调用次数。
+    """
+
+    interface_version = "1.0"
+
+    def __init__(self, task_id="H-9001"):
+        self.task_id = task_id
+        self.status = "running"
+        self.progress = 0
+        self.stage = "运行"
+        self.calls = []
+
+    def call_tool(self, name, args):
+        self.calls.append((name, dict(args or {})))
+        if name == "start_cleaning_task":
+            return {"ok": True, "task_id": self.task_id, "status": "queued"}
+        if name == "get_task_status":
+            return {"ok": True, "task_id": args.get("task_id"),
+                    "status": self.status, "stage": self.stage,
+                    "progress_percent": self.progress}
+        if name == "get_task_result":
+            if self.status != "succeeded":
+                return {"ok": False, "error": {
+                    "code": "TASK_NOT_FINISHED", "message": "任务尚未完成"}}
+            return {
+                "ok": True, "task_id": args.get("task_id"),
+                "counts": {"input_rows": 1150241, "output_rows": 1000209,
+                           "quarantined": 100830},
+                "scores": {"after": {"composite": 99.95}},
+                "note": "清洗与评分完成",
+                "artifacts": [
+                    {"path": "cleaned/movies/movies.dat", "kind": "data"},
+                    {"path": "report.json", "kind": "report"},
+                ],
+            }
+        if name == "list_schemes":
+            return {"ok": True, "schemes": []}
+        return {"ok": False, "error": {"code": "UNKNOWN", "message": name}}
+
+
+def _no_model():
+    """固定「无可用模型」：长任务终态走确定性收尾（不触模型、不连网）。"""
+    raise ModelError(NOT_CONFIGURED, "测试固定无模型，走确定性收尾")
+
+
+class TestRemoteLongTaskEndToEnd(unittest.TestCase):
+    def setUp(self):
+        self.tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="e2e_remote_")
+        self.client = FakeRemoteClient()
+        self.rt = Runtime(
+            db_path=os.path.join(self.tmp, "rt.sqlite"),
+            artifact_dir=os.path.join(self.tmp, "art"),
+            mcp_client=self.client,
+            task_probe=RemoteTaskProbe(self.client),
+            task_poll_interval=3600,
+            provider_factory=_no_model)
+        self.addCleanup(self.rt.close)
+        self.cid = self.rt.create_conversation(title="远端长任务端到端")
+
+    def test_full_path_start_status_terminal_result(self):
+        sub = self.rt.open_stream(self.cid)
+
+        # 1) 发起清洗（长任务）：经运行时工具后端 → 假 MCP 客户端
+        result = self.rt.run_offline(self.cid, "start_cleaning_task", {},
+                                     facts={}, enforce_policy=False)
+        self.assertTrue(result["ok"], result)
+        self.rt.long_tasks.stop_polling()      # 关掉后台轮询，改用受控 tick
+
+        self.assertEqual(self.client.calls[0][0], "start_cleaning_task")
+        records = self.rt.long_tasks.tasks_of(self.cid)
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(records))
+        record = records[0]
+        aid = record["agent_task_id"]
+        self.assertTrue(aid.startswith("LT-"))
+        self.assertEqual("H-9001", record["hadoop_task_id"])
+        self.assertEqual("running", record["status"])
+
+        # 2) 进度流动：查状态 → 进度/阶段同步进记录
+        self.client.progress, self.client.stage = 40, "清洗"
+        self.rt.long_tasks.poller.tick()
+        record = self.rt.long_tasks.get(self.cid, aid)
+        self.assertEqual("running", record["status"])
+        self.assertEqual(40, record["progress_percent"])
+        self.assertEqual("清洗", record["stage"])
+        self.assertIn(("get_task_status", {"task_id": "H-9001"}), self.client.calls)
+
+        # 3) 到终态：查状态 → 收 get_task_result → 进终态并推结果
+        self.client.status = "succeeded"
+        self.client.progress, self.client.stage = 100, "成功"
+        self.rt.long_tasks.poller.tick()
+        record = self.rt.long_tasks.get(self.cid, aid)
+        self.assertEqual("succeeded", record["status"])
+        self.assertEqual(100, record["progress_percent"])
+        self.assertIsInstance(record["result"], dict)
+        # 取结果是按 hadoop_task_id（双编号纪律）
+        self.assertIn(("get_task_result", {"task_id": "H-9001"}), self.client.calls)
+
+        # 4) 结果/产物真的产出
+        events = sub.drain()
+        kinds = [e["event"] for e in events]
+        self.assertIn(TASK_STATUS, kinds)
+        self.assertIn(RESULT_PAYLOAD, kinds)
+        self.assertIn(ARTIFACT_ADDED, kinds)
+        self.assertIn(MESSAGE_DONE, kinds)
+
+        payload = [e for e in events if e["event"] == RESULT_PAYLOAD][-1]["data"]
+        self.assertEqual("succeeded", payload["task"]["status"])
+        self.assertIn("data_volume", payload["blocks"])
+
+        paths = {a["path"] for a in self.rt.visible_artifacts(self.cid)}
+        self.assertIn("cleaned/movies/movies.dat", paths)
+        self.assertIn("report.json", paths)
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""/health 暴露远端连接状态（Task 13）的真实 HTTP 实测。
+
+用标准库 `http.client` 起真服务（`ThreadingHTTPServer`），注入只暴露 `_tool_backend`
+的桩运行时；断言：
+
+  * 配了 `AGENT_MCP_URL` / `AGENT_MCP_TOKEN` 时，`/health` 的 `mcp` 段 `token_set` 为真，
+    且**响应里绝不出现口令值**；
+  * 两个变量都没配时，`mcp` 段如实报告「不可用」，服务**不崩**；
+  * 注入的启动工具表一致性校验结果被带出（`ok/missing/extra/version_ok/peer_version`），
+    没有持有者时为 null。
+
+**不连网、不碰 127.0.0.1:8900。**
+"""
+import http.client
+import json
+import os
+import sys
+import threading
+import unittest
+
+AGENT_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+REPO_ROOT = os.path.dirname(AGENT_DIR)
+sys.path.insert(0, REPO_ROOT)
+
+from agent import http_api  # noqa: E402
+from agent.runtime import tool_exec  # noqa: E402
+
+TOKEN = "secret-token-value-should-never-appear"
+
+
+class _StubRuntime(object):
+    """只暴露 /health 需要的 `_tool_backend`（其余端点本测试不涉及）。"""
+
+    def __init__(self, tool_backend=None):
+        self._tool_backend = tool_backend
+
+
+class _FakeVerifyClient(object):
+    def __init__(self, result):
+        self._result = result
+
+    def verify_tools(self, declared):
+        return dict(self._result)
+
+
+class HealthRemoteTest(unittest.TestCase):
+    def setUp(self):
+        self._saved_runtime = http_api._RUNTIME
+        self.addCleanup(lambda: setattr(http_api, "_RUNTIME", self._saved_runtime))
+        self._saved_env = {k: os.environ.get(k)
+                           for k in ("AGENT_MCP_URL", "AGENT_MCP_TOKEN")}
+        self.addCleanup(self._restore_env)
+
+    def _restore_env(self):
+        for key, value in self._saved_env.items():
+            if value is None:
+                os.environ.pop(key, None)
+            else:
+                os.environ[key] = value
+
+    def _start(self, runtime):
+        srv = http_api.create_server("127.0.0.1", 0, runtime=runtime)
+        thread = threading.Thread(target=srv.serve_forever, daemon=True)
+        thread.start()
+        self.addCleanup(self._stop, srv, thread)
+        self.port = srv.server_address[1]
+
+    def _stop(self, srv, thread):
+        try:
+            srv.shutdown()
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+        try:
+            srv.server_close()
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+
+    def _health_raw(self):
+        conn = http.client.HTTPConnection("127.0.0.1", self.port, timeout=15)
+        try:
+            conn.request("GET", "/health")
+            resp = conn.getresponse()
+            raw = resp.read()
+            return resp.status, raw.decode("utf-8")
+        finally:
+            conn.close()
+
+    def test_health_reports_mcp_and_hides_token(self):
+        os.environ["AGENT_MCP_URL"] = "http://127.0.0.1:8900/mcp"
+        os.environ["AGENT_MCP_TOKEN"] = TOKEN
+        self._start(_StubRuntime())
+
+        status, raw = self._health_raw()
+        self.assertEqual(200, status)
+        body = json.loads(raw)
+        self.assertTrue(body["ok"])
+        self.assertIn("mcp", body)
+        self.assertTrue(body["mcp"]["token_set"])
+        self.assertTrue(body["mcp"]["usable"])
+        self.assertNotIn(TOKEN, raw)                 # 口令绝不外泄
+
+    def test_health_mcp_unusable_without_env(self):
+        os.environ.pop("AGENT_MCP_URL", None)
+        os.environ.pop("AGENT_MCP_TOKEN", None)
+        self._start(_StubRuntime())
+
+        status, raw = self._health_raw()
+        self.assertEqual(200, status)
+        body = json.loads(raw)
+        self.assertTrue(body["ok"])                  # 未接入也照常可用，不崩
+        self.assertIn("mcp", body)
+        self.assertFalse(body["mcp"]["usable"])
+        self.assertFalse(body["mcp"]["token_set"])
+
+    def test_health_surfaces_startup_tool_check(self):
+        os.environ.pop("AGENT_MCP_URL", None)
+        os.environ.pop("AGENT_MCP_TOKEN", None)
+        backend = tool_exec.build_tool_backend(_FakeVerifyClient({
+            "ok": False, "missing": ["cancel_task"], "extra": [],
+            "version_ok": True, "peer_version": "1.0"}))
+        tool_exec.wait_for_startup_tool_check(backend, timeout=5)
+        self._start(_StubRuntime(tool_backend=backend))
+
+        _, raw = self._health_raw()
+        check = json.loads(raw)["mcp"]["startup_check"]
+        self.assertIsNotNone(check)
+        self.assertFalse(check["ok"])
+        self.assertEqual(["cancel_task"], check["missing"])
+        self.assertEqual([], check["extra"])
+        self.assertTrue(check["version_ok"])
+        self.assertEqual("1.0", check["peer_version"])
+
+    def test_health_startup_check_null_without_holder(self):
+        os.environ.pop("AGENT_MCP_URL", None)
+        os.environ.pop("AGENT_MCP_TOKEN", None)
+        self._start(_StubRuntime())                  # 无后端 → 无持有者
+
+        _, raw = self._health_raw()
+        self.assertIsNone(json.loads(raw)["mcp"]["startup_check"])
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""真实 MCP 客户端（agent/mcp_http_client.py）单元测试。
+
+全部走注入的 `session_factory` 接缝：不连网、不起服务、不触模型。
+覆盖：列工具映射、调工具信封解析、非信封兜底、配置不可用、连接异常翻译、
+口令绝不外泄、以及基类接口（版本协商 / 关闭）。
+"""
+import json
+import os
+import sys
+
+AGENT_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+REPO_ROOT = os.path.dirname(AGENT_DIR)
+sys.path.insert(0, REPO_ROOT)
+
+from agent import mcp_contract  # noqa: E402
+from agent import mcp_http_client as mc  # noqa: E402
+
+
+def cfg(url="http://127.0.0.1:8900/mcp", token="t" * 20):
+    return mc.McpConfig({"url": url, "token": token})
+
+
+class FakeSession:
+    """假会话：不连网，按预置结果返回，并记录调用。"""
+
+    def __init__(self, tools=None, call_result=None, call_text=None):
+        self._tools = tools or []
+        self._call_result = call_result
+        self._call_text = call_text
+        self.calls = []
+
+    async def initialize(self):
+        return None
+
+    async def list_tools(self):
+        class _T:
+            def __init__(self, name, schema):
+                self.name = name
+                self.title = name
+                self.input_schema = schema
+
+        return type("R", (), {"tools": [_T(n, s) for n, s in self._tools]})()
+
+    async def call_tool(self, name, args):
+        class _C:
+            def __init__(self, text):
+                self.text = text
+
+        self.calls.append((name, dict(args or {})))
+        text = (self._call_text if self._call_text is not None
+                else json.dumps(self._call_result))
+        return type("R", (), {"content": [_C(text)]})()
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# 列工具
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+def test_list_tools_maps_to_rows():
+    client = mc.McpHttpClient(cfg(), session_factory=lambda: FakeSession(
+        tools=[("list_schemes", {"type": "object", "properties": {}, "required": []})]))
+    rows = client.list_tools()
+    assert rows[0]["name"] == "list_schemes"
+    assert rows[0]["source"] == "mcp"
+    assert rows[0]["parameters"]["type"] == "object"
+
+
+def test_list_tools_unusable_returns_empty():
+    client = mc.McpHttpClient(mc.McpConfig({}))
+    assert client.list_tools() == []
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# 调工具：信封解析
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+def test_call_tool_returns_envelope():
+    client = mc.McpHttpClient(cfg(), session_factory=lambda: FakeSession(
+        call_result={"ok": True, "interface_version": "1.0", "task_id": "T1"}))
+    env = client.call_tool("get_task_status", {"task_id": "T1"})
+    assert env["ok"] is True and env["task_id"] == "T1"
+
+
+def test_call_tool_passes_name_and_args():
+    session = FakeSession(call_result={"ok": True})
+    client = mc.McpHttpClient(cfg(), session_factory=lambda: session)
+    client.call_tool("get_samples", {"task_id": "H1", "limit": 3})
+    assert session.calls == [("get_samples", {"task_id": "H1", "limit": 3})]
+
+
+def test_call_tool_non_json_is_bad_output():
+    client = mc.McpHttpClient(cfg(), session_factory=lambda: FakeSession(call_text="not json"))
+    env = client.call_tool("x", {})
+    assert env["ok"] is False
+    assert env["error"]["code"] == mcp_contract.CODE_DRIVER_BAD_OUTPUT
+
+
+def test_call_tool_non_object_is_bad_output():
+    client = mc.McpHttpClient(cfg(), session_factory=lambda: FakeSession(call_text="[1, 2, 3]"))
+    env = client.call_tool("x", {})
+    assert env["ok"] is False
+    assert env["error"]["code"] == mcp_contract.CODE_DRIVER_BAD_OUTPUT
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# 配置不可用：一律结构化 MCP_UNAVAILABLE，绝不连网
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+def test_unusable_config_reports_unavailable():
+    client = mc.McpHttpClient(mc.McpConfig({}))
+    env = client.call_tool("list_schemes", {})
+    assert env["ok"] is False
+    assert env["error"]["code"] == "MCP_UNAVAILABLE"
+
+
+def test_unusable_config_status_describe_health_all_unavailable():
+    client = mc.McpHttpClient(mc.McpConfig({}))
+    for env in (client.get_task_status("T1"), client.describe_capabilities(),
+                client.health()):
+        assert env["ok"] is False
+        assert env["error"]["code"] == "MCP_UNAVAILABLE"
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# 连接 / 协议异常：翻成可读说明，且绝不带口令
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+def test_connection_refused_is_explained_and_token_hidden():
+    def boom():
+        raise ConnectionRefusedError("connection refused")
+
+    client = mc.McpHttpClient(cfg(token="super-secret-token"), session_factory=boom)
+    env = client.call_tool("x", {})
+    assert env["ok"] is False
+    assert env["error"]["code"] == mcp_contract.CODE_DRIVER_UNREACHABLE
+    assert "连不上" in env["error"]["message"]
+    assert "super-secret-token" not in env["error"]["message"]
+
+
+def test_http_401_is_explained_as_credential_problem():
+    def boom():
+        raise RuntimeError("unexpected status: 401 Unauthorized")
+
+    client = mc.McpHttpClient(cfg(token="super-secret-token"), session_factory=boom)
+    env = client.call_tool("x", {})
+    assert env["error"]["code"] == mcp_contract.CODE_DRIVER_UNREACHABLE
+    assert "凭据" in env["error"]["message"]
+    assert "super-secret-token" not in env["error"]["message"]
+
+
+def test_generic_error_is_explained():
+    def boom():
+        raise RuntimeError("some other protocol failure")
+
+    client = mc.McpHttpClient(cfg(), session_factory=boom)
+    env = client.call_tool("x", {})
+    assert env["error"]["code"] == mcp_contract.CODE_DRIVER_UNREACHABLE
+    assert "调用失败" in env["error"]["message"]
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# 便捷方法
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+def test_get_task_status_uses_task_id():
+    client = mc.McpHttpClient(cfg(), session_factory=lambda: FakeSession(
+        call_result={"ok": True, "task_id": "H1", "status": "running"}))
+    env = client.get_task_status("H1")
+    assert env["task_id"] == "H1" and env["status"] == "running"
+
+
+def test_describe_capabilities_calls_tool():
+    client = mc.McpHttpClient(cfg(), session_factory=lambda: FakeSession(
+        call_result={"ok": True, "tools": []}))
+    assert client.describe_capabilities()["ok"] is True
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# 基类契约：接口版本来自配置，版本协商 / 关闭沿用基类
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+def test_interface_version_from_config():
+    c = mc.McpConfig({"url": "http://x/mcp", "token": "t" * 20,
+                      "interface_version": "1.0"})
+    assert mc.McpHttpClient(c).interface_version == "1.0"
+
+
+def test_negotiate_version_and_close_inherited():
+    client = mc.McpHttpClient(cfg())
+    assert client.negotiate_version("1.9")["compatible"] is True
+    assert client.close() is None

+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""远端能力服务连接配置（Task 1）：默认不可用、环境变量覆盖、口令脱敏。
+
+红线：口令绝不进任何用户可见输出；`safe_dict()` 只暴露是否已配置。
+"""
+import os
+import sys
+
+AGENT_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+REPO_ROOT = os.path.dirname(AGENT_DIR)
+sys.path.insert(0, REPO_ROOT)
+
+from agent import mcp_config  # noqa: E402
+
+
+def test_defaults_unusable_without_url_and_token(monkeypatch):
+    for k in ("AGENT_MCP_URL", "AGENT_MCP_TOKEN"):
+        monkeypatch.delenv(k, raising=False)
+    cfg = mcp_config.load()
+    assert cfg.usable is False
+    assert cfg.interface_version == "1.0"
+
+
+def test_env_overrides_and_usable(monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("AGENT_MCP_URL", "http://127.0.0.1:8900/mcp")
+    monkeypatch.setenv("AGENT_MCP_TOKEN", "x" * 20)
+    cfg = mcp_config.load()
+    assert cfg.usable is True
+    assert cfg.protocol_url() == "http://127.0.0.1:8900/mcp"
+    assert cfg.health_url() == "http://127.0.0.1:8900/health"
+
+
+def test_safe_dict_hides_token(monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("AGENT_MCP_URL", "http://127.0.0.1:8900/mcp")
+    monkeypatch.setenv("AGENT_MCP_TOKEN", "secret-token-value")
+    d = mcp_config.load().safe_dict()
+    assert "secret-token-value" not in str(d)
+    assert d["token_set"] is True

+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""远端两段只读下载路由的带口令流式取回（`agent/mcp_download.py`）单元测试。
+
+全部用 `httpx.MockTransport` 注入：**不连网、不碰 127.0.0.1:8900**。
+覆盖：成功透传字节 + 下载头、结构化错误码原样保留、口令只进请求头（不外泄）、
+未接入时结构化 `MCP_UNAVAILABLE`；以及 `http_api` 的中转处理（假客户端 + 假传输）。
+"""
+import http.client
+import io
+import json
+import os
+import sys
+import threading
+import unittest
+
+import httpx
+
+AGENT_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+REPO_ROOT = os.path.dirname(AGENT_DIR)
+sys.path.insert(0, REPO_ROOT)
+
+from agent import http_api  # noqa: E402
+from agent import mcp_download as dl  # noqa: E402
+from agent.mcp_config import McpConfig  # noqa: E402
+from agent.mcp_contract import NullMcpClient  # noqa: E402
+
+TOKEN = "t" * 20
+
+
+def cfg(transport=None):
+    c = McpConfig({"url": "http://hadoop:8765/mcp", "token": TOKEN})
+    if transport is not None:
+        c.transport = transport                      # 仅测试注入：不连网
+    return c
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# 模块层：URL / 字节 / 下载头 / 错误码
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+def test_open_task_stream_relays_bytes_and_headers():
+    seen = {}
+
+    def handler(request):
+        seen["url"] = str(request.url)
+        seen["auth"] = request.headers.get("Authorization")
+        assert str(request.url) == (
+            "http://hadoop:8765/download/task/H1/cleaned/x/ratings.dat")
+        return httpx.Response(
+            200, content=b"hello",
+            headers={"content-disposition": 'attachment; filename="a.dat"',
+                     "content-length": "5"})
+
+    it, headers, err = dl.open_task_stream(
+        cfg(), "H1", "cleaned/x/ratings.dat",
+        transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
+    assert err is None
+    assert b"".join(it) == b"hello"
+    assert "filename" in headers.get("Content-Disposition", "")
+    assert headers["Content-Length"] == "5"
+    assert seen["auth"] == "Bearer " + TOKEN
+    assert seen["url"] == "http://hadoop:8765/download/task/H1/cleaned/x/ratings.dat"
+
+
+def test_open_returns_iterator_for_streaming():
+    """成功时给的是**迭代器**（流式转发），不是已经读进内存的 bytes。"""
+    def handler(request):
+        return httpx.Response(
+            200, content=b"a" * 100,
+            headers={"content-disposition": 'attachment; filename="x"'})
+
+    it, _headers, err = dl.open_task_stream(cfg(), "H1", "x",
+                                            transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
+    assert err is None
+    assert hasattr(it, "__next__")
+    assert not isinstance(it, (bytes, bytearray))
+    assert b"".join(it) == b"a" * 100
+
+
+def test_open_task_stream_surfaces_structured_error():
+    def handler(request):
+        return httpx.Response(404, json={
+            "ok": False,
+            "error": {"code": "FILE_NOT_FOUND", "message": "文件不存在",
+                      "path": "nope"}})
+
+    it, headers, err = dl.open_task_stream(cfg(), "H1", "nope",
+                                           transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
+    assert it is None and headers is None
+    assert err["code"] == "FILE_NOT_FOUND"
+    assert err["message"] == "文件不存在"
+    assert err["path"] == "nope"                     # 远端细节原样带出
+
+
+def test_forbidden_path_code_preserved_and_status_mapped():
+    """服务端对**本机已放行**的路径回越界信封时，原样保留码与细节（不降级）。"""
+    seen = {}
+
+    def handler(request):
+        seen["url"] = str(request.url)
+        return httpx.Response(400, json={
+            "ok": False,
+            "error": {"code": "FORBIDDEN_PATH", "message": "路径越界",
+                      "reason_kind": "residue"}})
+
+    _it, _headers, err = dl.open_task_stream(
+        cfg(), "H1", "x",
+        transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
+    assert seen["url"] == "http://hadoop:8765/download/task/H1/x"  # 请求确已发出
+    assert err["code"] == "FORBIDDEN_PATH"           # 不降级为泛化错误
+    assert err["reason_kind"] == "residue"
+    assert dl.status_for_code(err["code"]) == 400
+
+
+def test_task_traversal_rejected_locally_without_request():
+    """相对穿越 `..` 段：本机拒，**不发任何请求**（httpx 归一化会吞掉路由段）。"""
+    def handler(request):
+        raise AssertionError("越界路径不应发起任何请求")
+
+    it, headers, err = dl.open_task_stream(
+        cfg(), "H1", "../../etc/passwd",
+        transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
+    assert it is None and headers is None
+    assert err == {"code": "FORBIDDEN_PATH", "message": "该路径不允许访问"}
+    assert dl.status_for_code(err["code"]) == 400
+
+
+def test_task_other_forbidden_paths_rejected_locally_without_request():
+    """绝对路径 / 反斜杠 / NUL / 中间 `..` 都在本机拒，请求不发。"""
+    def handler(request):
+        raise AssertionError("非法路径不应发起任何请求")
+
+    for bad in ("/etc/passwd", "a\\b", "a\x00b", "cleaned/../x", ".."):
+        it, headers, err = dl.open_task_stream(
+            cfg(), "H1", bad, transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
+        assert it is None and headers is None
+        assert err["code"] == "FORBIDDEN_PATH", bad
+
+
+def test_published_forbidden_segments_rejected_locally_without_request():
+    """已发布下载：`data_version`/`table` 须是单一分段，越界分段本机拒。"""
+    def handler(request):
+        raise AssertionError("越界版本/表名不应发起任何请求")
+
+    for dv, tbl in (("ml1m/v1", "ratings"), ("ml1m-clean-v1", "../ratings"),
+                    ("..", "ratings"), ("ml1m-clean-v1", "a/b"),
+                    ("ml1m-clean-v1", "a\\b")):
+        it, headers, err = dl.open_published_stream(
+            cfg(), dv, tbl, transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
+        assert it is None and headers is None
+        assert err["code"] == "FORBIDDEN_PATH", (dv, tbl)
+
+
+def test_unauthorized_maps_to_401():
+    def handler(request):
+        return httpx.Response(401, json={
+            "ok": False, "error": {"code": "UNAUTHORIZED", "message": "口令不对"}})
+
+    _it, _headers, err = dl.open_task_stream(cfg(), "H1", "x",
+                                             transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
+    assert err["code"] == "UNAUTHORIZED"
+    assert dl.status_for_code(err["code"]) == 401
+
+
+def test_open_published_stream_url_and_bytes():
+    def handler(request):
+        assert str(request.url) == (
+            "http://hadoop:8765/download/published/ml1m-clean-v1/ratings")
+        return httpx.Response(
+            200, content=b"data",
+            headers={"content-disposition": 'attachment; filename="ratings.dat"'})
+
+    it, headers, err = dl.open_published_stream(
+        cfg(), "ml1m-clean-v1", "ratings", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
+    assert err is None
+    assert b"".join(it) == b"data"
+    assert "ratings.dat" in headers.get("Content-Disposition", "")
+
+
+def test_unusable_config_reports_unavailable_without_network():
+    def handler(request):                            # 永不调用
+        raise AssertionError("未接入时不应发起任何请求")
+
+    it, headers, err = dl.open_task_stream(
+        McpConfig({}), "H1", "x", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
+    assert it is None and headers is None
+    assert err["code"] == "MCP_UNAVAILABLE"
+
+
+def test_token_never_leaks_into_error():
+    def handler(request):
+        return httpx.Response(500, content=b"boom")
+
+    _it, _headers, err = dl.open_task_stream(
+        cfg(), "H1", "x", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
+    assert TOKEN not in json.dumps(err, ensure_ascii=False)
+    assert "Bearer" not in json.dumps(err, ensure_ascii=False)
+
+
+class _Recorder(object):
+    """`_stream_response` 的最小 `self` 替身（无 socket），记录写出的字节。"""
+
+    def __init__(self):
+        self.wfile = io.BytesIO()
+        self.close_connection = False
+        self.headers = []
+        self.code = None
+
+    def send_response(self, code):
+        self.code = code
+
+    def send_header(self, key, value):
+        self.headers.append((key, value))
+
+    def _cors(self):
+        pass
+
+    def end_headers(self):
+        pass
+
+    def _flush(self):
+        pass
+
+
+def test_stream_response_swallows_upstream_read_error():
+    """远端转发中途断开（httpx.ReadError）不得从处理函数里冒出来。"""
+    def gen():
+        yield b"first-"
+        raise httpx.ReadError("upstream dropped")
+
+    rec = _Recorder()
+    http_api.Handler._stream_response(rec, gen(), {})
+    assert rec.wfile.getvalue() == b"first-"
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# http_api 中转处理：假客户端 + 假传输（真起服务，但不连远端）
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+class FakeClient(object):
+    def __init__(self, config):
+        self.config = config
+
+
+class StubRuntime(object):
+    def __init__(self, client):
+        self.mcp_client = client
+
+
+class RelayServerBase(unittest.TestCase):
+    def setUp(self):
+        self._saved_runtime = http_api._RUNTIME
+        self.addCleanup(lambda: setattr(http_api, "_RUNTIME", self._saved_runtime))
+        srv = http_api.create_server("127.0.0.1", 0, runtime=self._runtime())
+        thread = threading.Thread(target=srv.serve_forever, daemon=True)
+        thread.start()
+        self.addCleanup(self._stop, srv, thread)
+        self.port = srv.server_address[1]
+
+    def _runtime(self):                              # pragma: no cover - 子类覆盖
+        raise NotImplementedError
+
+    def _stop(self, srv, thread):
+        try:
+            srv.shutdown()
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+        try:
+            srv.server_close()
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+
+    def request(self, path):
+        conn = http.client.HTTPConnection("127.0.0.1", self.port, timeout=20)
+        try:
+            conn.request("GET", path)
+            resp = conn.getresponse()
+            raw = resp.read()
+            return resp.status, dict(resp.getheaders()), raw
+        finally:
+            conn.close()
+
+
+class RelayHandlerTest(RelayServerBase):
+    def _runtime(self):
+        def handler(request):
+            assert request.headers["Authorization"] == "Bearer " + TOKEN
+            if "forbidden" in str(request.url):
+                return httpx.Response(403, json={
+                    "ok": False,
+                    "error": {"code": "FORBIDDEN_HOST", "message": "Host 不在允许列表"}})
+            return httpx.Response(
+                200, content=b"clean-ratings",
+                headers={"content-disposition": 'attachment; filename="ratings.dat"',
+                         "content-length": "13"})
+
+        self._config = cfg(httpx.MockTransport(handler))
+        return StubRuntime(FakeClient(self._config))
+
+    def test_download_relays_bytes_and_headers(self):
+        status, headers, raw = self.request(
+            "/api/files/download?task_id=H1&path=cleaned/x/ratings.dat")
+        self.assertEqual(status, 200)
+        self.assertEqual(raw, b"clean-ratings")
+        self.assertIn("ratings.dat", headers.get("Content-Disposition", ""))
+        self.assertEqual(headers.get("Content-Length"), "13")
+
+    def test_download_error_returns_envelope_and_status(self):
+        status, _headers, raw = self.request(
+            "/api/files/download?task_id=H1&path=forbidden")
+        self.assertEqual(status, 403)
+        body = json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"))
+        self.assertFalse(body["ok"])
+        self.assertEqual(body["error"]["code"], "FORBIDDEN_HOST")
+
+    def test_published_download_relays(self):
+        status, headers, raw = self.request(
+            "/api/data/published/download?data_version=ml1m-clean-v1&table=ratings")
+        self.assertEqual(status, 200)
+        self.assertEqual(raw, b"clean-ratings")
+        self.assertIn("ratings.dat", headers.get("Content-Disposition", ""))
+
+
+class UnconfiguredRelayTest(RelayServerBase):
+    def _runtime(self):
+        return StubRuntime(NullMcpClient())
+
+    def test_null_client_reports_mcp_unavailable(self):
+        status, _headers, raw = self.request("/api/files/download?task_id=H1&path=x")
+        self.assertEqual(status, 503)
+        body = json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"))
+        self.assertFalse(body["ok"])
+        self.assertEqual(body["error"]["code"], "MCP_UNAVAILABLE")
+
+    def test_published_unavailable(self):
+        status, _headers, raw = self.request(
+            "/api/data/published/download?data_version=v1&table=ratings")
+        self.assertEqual(status, 503)
+        self.assertEqual(json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"))["error"]["code"],
+                         "MCP_UNAVAILABLE")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""结构化错误码扩充与细类单测（Task 5）。
+
+- `STRUCTURED_ERROR_CODES` 收录远端可能出现的全部结构化码（本端词汇表对齐）；
+- `REASON_KINDS` 与远端读取层共用一套细类取值；
+- `describe_error(error)` 生成面向用户的**可读中文**说明，回退顺序为：
+  已知码 → 远端 `error.message` → 原始码；文案不得出现「冒烟/对账/口径」。
+- `normalize_error` 语义无损：未知码原样透传，既有映射不变。
+"""
+import os
+import sys
+import unittest
+
+AGENT_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+REPO_ROOT = os.path.dirname(AGENT_DIR)
+sys.path.insert(0, REPO_ROOT)
+
+from agent import mcp_contract as c  # noqa: E402
+
+
+NEW_CODES = (
+    "ABORTED",
+    "FILE_NOT_FOUND",
+    "FORBIDDEN_PATH",
+    "CONTENT_TOO_LARGE",
+    "PRODUCT_INVALID",
+    "CANCEL_FAILED",
+    "UNAUTHORIZED",
+    "FORBIDDEN_HOST",
+    "WAIT_LIMIT_REACHED",
+    "INTERNAL_ERROR",
+    "NOT_PREVIEWABLE",
+)
+
+EXISTING_CODES = (
+    "USAGE",
+    "TASK_FAILED",
+    "TASK_NOT_FOUND",
+    "TASK_NOT_FINISHED",
+    "VERSION_CONFLICT",
+    "DEPENDENCY_MISSING",
+    "TASK_ALREADY_RUNNING",
+    "DRIVER_NO_OUTPUT",
+    "DRIVER_BAD_OUTPUT",
+    "DRIVER_UNREACHABLE",
+    "DRIVER_TIMEOUT",
+    "MCP_UNAVAILABLE",
+)
+
+FORBIDDEN_WORDS = ("冒烟", "对账", "口径")
+
+
+class TestStructuredErrorCodes(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_new_codes_present(self):
+        for code in NEW_CODES:
+            with self.subTest(code=code):
+                self.assertIn(code, c.STRUCTURED_ERROR_CODES)
+
+    def test_existing_codes_kept(self):
+        for code in EXISTING_CODES:
+            with self.subTest(code=code):
+                self.assertIn(code, c.STRUCTURED_ERROR_CODES)
+
+
+class TestReasonKinds(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_reason_kinds_exact_set(self):
+        self.assertEqual(
+            {"control", "sidecar", "residue", "directory", "not_regular_file"},
+            set(c.REASON_KINDS))
+        self.assertEqual(
+            ("control", "sidecar", "residue", "directory", "not_regular_file"),
+            tuple(c.REASON_KINDS))
+
+
+class TestDescribeError(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_known_code_gives_readable_chinese(self):
+        text = c.describe_error({"code": "TASK_NOT_FOUND", "message": "raw-en-body"})
+        self.assertIsInstance(text, str)
+        self.assertTrue(text.strip())
+        self.assertIn("任务", text)
+        # 已知码优先本端说明，不直接回退到远端 message / 原始码
+        self.assertNotEqual("TASK_NOT_FOUND", text)
+        self.assertNotIn("raw-en-body", text)
+
+    def test_known_code_as_bare_string(self):
+        self.assertEqual(c.describe_error("ABORTED"), c.describe_error({"code": "ABORTED"}))
+
+    def test_unknown_code_falls_back_to_message(self):
+        text = c.describe_error({"code": "SOME_FUTURE_CODE", "message": "远端给的说明"})
+        self.assertEqual("远端给的说明", text)
+
+    def test_unknown_code_without_message_returns_raw_code(self):
+        self.assertEqual("SOME_FUTURE_CODE", c.describe_error({"code": "SOME_FUTURE_CODE"}))
+        self.assertEqual("SOME_FUTURE_CODE", c.describe_error("SOME_FUTURE_CODE"))
+
+    def test_empty_or_missing_is_readable_fallback(self):
+        for value in ({}, None, "", {"message": ""}):
+            with self.subTest(value=value):
+                text = c.describe_error(value)
+                self.assertIsInstance(text, str)
+                self.assertTrue(text.strip())
+
+    def test_known_driver_native_code_has_explanation(self):
+        self.assertEqual(c.describe_error({"code": "CONFIG_INVALID"}),
+                         c.describe_error("CONFIG_INVALID"))
+        self.assertNotEqual("CONFIG_INVALID", c.describe_error({"code": "CONFIG_INVALID"}))
+
+    def test_all_known_codes_are_readable_and_wording_safe(self):
+        for code in c.STRUCTURED_ERROR_CODES + getattr(c, "DRIVER_NATIVE_CODES", ()):
+            with self.subTest(code=code):
+                text = c.describe_error({"code": code})
+                self.assertTrue(text.strip())
+                self.assertNotEqual(code, text)
+                for bad in FORBIDDEN_WORDS:
+                    self.assertNotIn(bad, text)
+
+
+class TestNormalizeSemanticsPreserving(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_new_structured_code_passes_through_unchanged(self):
+        err = {"code": "FILE_NOT_FOUND", "message": "不存在"}
+        out = c.normalize_error(err)
+        self.assertEqual("FILE_NOT_FOUND", out["code"])
+        self.assertEqual("不存在", out["message"])
+
+    def test_unknown_code_still_passes_through(self):
+        out = c.normalize_error({"code": "TOTALLY_UNKNOWN", "message": "x"})
+        self.assertEqual("TOTALLY_UNKNOWN", out["code"])
+
+    def test_existing_exit_mapping_unchanged(self):
+        out = c.normalize_error({"code": "SOME_DRIVER_CODE"},
+                                exit_code=2)  # EXIT_USAGE
+        self.assertEqual("USAGE", out["code"])
+        self.assertEqual("SOME_DRIVER_CODE", out.get("driver_code"))
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()
