+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""远端工具表单测：22 项远端能力改判 source=mcp，本机仅留快速演示。
+
+来源权威：`hadoop/mcpserver/tools.py` 的 `TOOLS`（能力自描述）。本表是 Agent
+侧的权威形状；参数名/默认值/枚举/取值范围必须与远端一致。
+"""
+import json
+import os
+import sys
+import unittest
+
+AGENT_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+REPO_ROOT = os.path.dirname(AGENT_DIR)
+sys.path.insert(0, REPO_ROOT)
+
+from agent import mcp_http_client as mc  # noqa: E402
+from agent import tool_specs, mcp_tools  # noqa: E402
+
+
+class TestRemoteToolSpecs(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_remote_tool_count_and_source(self):
+        self.assertEqual(22, len(mcp_tools.REMOTE_TOOL_SPECS))
+        for name, spec in mcp_tools.REMOTE_TOOL_SPECS.items():
+            with self.subTest(tool=name):
+                self.assertEqual("mcp", spec["source"])
+                self.assertEqual(name, spec["name"])
+
+    def test_remote_tool_names(self):
+        self.assertEqual(22, len(mcp_tools.remote_tool_names()))
+        self.assertEqual(tuple(mcp_tools.REMOTE_TOOL_SPECS.keys()),
+                         mcp_tools.remote_tool_names())
+
+    def test_spec_for(self):
+        self.assertIs(mcp_tools.REMOTE_TOOL_SPECS["get_samples"],
+                      mcp_tools.spec_for("get_samples"))
+        self.assertIsNone(mcp_tools.spec_for("quick_clean_demo"))
+        self.assertIsNone(mcp_tools.spec_for("no_such_tool"))
+
+
+class TestLocalOnly(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_local_only_is_quick_demo(self):
+        self.assertEqual(("quick_clean_demo",), mcp_tools.LOCAL_ONLY_TOOLS)
+
+    def test_tool_specs_routes_shared_tools_to_mcp(self):
+        self.assertEqual("mcp", tool_specs.source_for("get_samples"))
+        self.assertEqual("mcp", tool_specs.source_for("start_cleaning_task"))
+        self.assertEqual("mcp", tool_specs.source_for("score_task"))
+        self.assertEqual("local", tool_specs.source_for("quick_clean_demo"))
+
+    def test_only_quick_demo_is_local(self):
+        local = [n for n in tool_specs.tool_names()
+                 if tool_specs.source_for(n) == "local"]
+        self.assertEqual(["quick_clean_demo"], local)
+
+
+class TestParamAlignment(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_samples_uses_limit_not_n(self):
+        props = mcp_tools.REMOTE_TOOL_SPECS["get_samples"]["parameters"]["properties"]
+        self.assertIn("limit", props)
+        self.assertNotIn("n", props)
+        self.assertEqual(5, props["limit"]["default"])
+        self.assertEqual(0, props["limit"]["minimum"])
+        self.assertEqual(2000, props["limit"]["maximum"])
+
+    def test_samples_defaults_match_remote(self):
+        props = mcp_tools.REMOTE_TOOL_SPECS["get_samples"]["parameters"]["properties"]
+        self.assertEqual("cleaned", props["sample_type"]["default"])
+        self.assertEqual(["cleaned", "quarantine"], props["sample_type"]["enum"])
+        self.assertEqual("movies", props["table"]["default"])
+        self.assertEqual(["movies", "ratings", "users"], props["table"]["enum"])
+
+    def test_report_uses_format_not_fmt(self):
+        props = mcp_tools.REMOTE_TOOL_SPECS["get_report"]["parameters"]["properties"]
+        self.assertIn("format", props)
+        self.assertNotIn("fmt", props)
+        self.assertEqual("json", props["format"]["default"])
+        self.assertEqual(["md", "json"], props["format"]["enum"])
+
+    def test_no_foreground_or_exec_mode_on_start_tools(self):
+        for name in ("start_cleaning_task", "start_clean_task", "score_task"):
+            with self.subTest(tool=name):
+                props = mcp_tools.REMOTE_TOOL_SPECS[name]["parameters"]["properties"]
+                self.assertNotIn("foreground", props)
+                self.assertNotIn("exec_mode", props)
+
+    def test_score_task_requires_source(self):
+        spec = mcp_tools.REMOTE_TOOL_SPECS["score_task"]
+        self.assertEqual(["source"], spec["parameters"]["required"])
+        self.assertEqual(["raw", "published", "task"],
+                         spec["parameters"]["properties"]["source"]["enum"])
+
+    def test_long_running_flags_and_estimates(self):
+        expected = {"start_cleaning_task": 1500, "start_clean_task": 900,
+                    "score_task": 300, "wait_for_task": 300}
+        for name, seconds in expected.items():
+            with self.subTest(tool=name):
+                spec = mcp_tools.REMOTE_TOOL_SPECS[name]
+                self.assertTrue(spec["long_running"])
+                self.assertEqual(seconds, spec["estimated_seconds"])
+        # 只读工具非长耗时
+        self.assertFalse(mcp_tools.REMOTE_TOOL_SPECS["get_samples"]["long_running"])
+
+    def test_stream_fallback_matches_remote_estimates(self):
+        """兜底映射（无规格时用）不得与远端规格矛盾（1500 / 900 / 300）。"""
+        from agent.runtime import stream
+        for name in ("start_cleaning_task", "start_clean_task", "score_task"):
+            with self.subTest(tool=name):
+                self.assertEqual(
+                    mcp_tools.REMOTE_TOOL_SPECS[name]["estimated_seconds"],
+                    stream.LONG_RUNNING_TOOLS[name])
+
+
+class _CapsSession:
+    """假会话：`describe_capabilities` 返回预置信封（不连网）。"""
+
+    def __init__(self, envelope):
+        self._envelope = envelope
+        self.calls = []
+
+    async def initialize(self):
+        return None
+
+    async def call_tool(self, name, args):
+        self.calls.append((name, dict(args or {})))
+
+        class _C:
+            def __init__(self, text):
+                self.text = text
+
+        return type("R", (), {"content": [_C(json.dumps(self._envelope))]})()
+
+
+def _caps_envelope(names, version="1.0"):
+    """仿远端 `describe_capabilities` 信封：`tools` 为公开条目列表。"""
+    return {"ok": True, "interface_version": version,
+            "tools": [{"name": name} for name in names],
+            "declared_count": len(list(names))}
+
+
+def _client_with(envelope, url="http://127.0.0.1:8900/mcp", token="t" * 20):
+    cfg = mc.McpConfig({"url": url, "token": token})
+    return mc.McpHttpClient(cfg, session_factory=lambda: _CapsSession(envelope))
+
+
+class TestVerifyTools(unittest.TestCase):
+    """启动时「工具表 ↔ 能力自描述」一致性校验（Task 4）。"""
+
+    def test_exact_match_is_ok(self):
+        names = mcp_tools.remote_tool_names()
+        out = _client_with(_caps_envelope(names)).verify_tools(names)
+        self.assertTrue(out["ok"])
+        self.assertEqual([], out["missing"])
+        self.assertEqual([], out["extra"])
+        self.assertTrue(out["version_ok"])
+        self.assertEqual("1.0", out["peer_version"])
+
+    def test_declared_but_absent_on_server_is_missing(self):
+        server = list(mcp_tools.remote_tool_names())
+        declared = set(server) | {"declared_only_tool"}    # 多声明了一个远端没有的
+        out = _client_with(_caps_envelope(server)).verify_tools(declared)
+        self.assertFalse(out["ok"])
+        self.assertIn("declared_only_tool", out["missing"])
+        self.assertEqual([], out["extra"])
+
+    def test_on_server_but_not_declared_is_extra(self):
+        server = list(mcp_tools.remote_tool_names())
+        declared = set(server) - {"cancel_task"}          # 少声明了一个
+        out = _client_with(_caps_envelope(server)).verify_tools(declared)
+        self.assertFalse(out["ok"])
+        self.assertIn("cancel_task", out["extra"])
+        self.assertEqual([], out["missing"])
+
+    def test_same_major_version_is_ok_even_if_minor_differs(self):
+        names = mcp_tools.remote_tool_names()
+        out = _client_with(_caps_envelope(names, version="1.7")).verify_tools(names)
+        self.assertTrue(out["version_ok"])
+        self.assertTrue(out["ok"])
+        self.assertEqual("1.7", out["peer_version"])
+
+    def test_major_version_mismatch_is_not_ok(self):
+        names = mcp_tools.remote_tool_names()
+        out = _client_with(_caps_envelope(names, version="2.0")).verify_tools(names)
+        self.assertFalse(out["version_ok"])
+        self.assertFalse(out["ok"])
+        self.assertEqual("2.0", out["peer_version"])
+
+    def test_server_failure_envelope_explains_and_does_not_raise(self):
+        client = _client_with({"ok": False, "interface_version": "1.0",
+                               "error": {"code": "INTERNAL_ERROR",
+                                         "message": "远端内部错误"}})
+        out = client.verify_tools(mcp_tools.remote_tool_names())
+        self.assertFalse(out["ok"])
+        self.assertIn("远端内部错误", out["reason"])
+
+    def test_unusable_config_is_not_ok_without_networking(self):
+        client = mc.McpHttpClient(mc.McpConfig({}))
+        out = client.verify_tools(mcp_tools.remote_tool_names())
+        self.assertFalse(out["ok"])
+        self.assertIn("reason", out)
+        self.assertEqual([], out["missing"])
+        self.assertEqual([], out["extra"])
+
+
+class TestStartupToolCheck(unittest.TestCase):
+    """启动装配点的非阻断校验（Task 4）：结果按后端实例持有（非模块级单槽）。"""
+
+    def test_build_backend_records_verification(self):
+        from agent.runtime import tool_exec
+
+        class _Client:
+            def verify_tools(self, declared):
+                return {"ok": True, "missing": [], "extra": [],
+                        "version_ok": True, "peer_version": "1.0"}
+
+        backend = tool_exec.build_tool_backend(_Client())
+        out = tool_exec.wait_for_startup_tool_check(backend, timeout=5)
+        self.assertTrue(out["ok"])
+        self.assertTrue(tool_exec.startup_tool_check(backend)["ok"])
+
+    def test_build_backend_is_non_blocking_on_failure(self):
+        from agent.runtime import tool_exec
+
+        class _Boom:
+            def verify_tools(self, declared):
+                raise RuntimeError("server absent")
+
+        backend = tool_exec.build_tool_backend(_Boom())   # 装配照常返回，不抛
+        self.assertIsNotNone(backend)
+        out = tool_exec.wait_for_startup_tool_check(backend, timeout=5)
+        self.assertFalse(out["ok"])
+        self.assertIn("reason", out)
+
+    def test_startup_check_is_owned_per_backend(self):
+        from agent.runtime import tool_exec
+
+        class _Client:
+            def __init__(self, ok):
+                self._ok = ok
+
+            def verify_tools(self, declared):
+                return {"ok": self._ok, "missing": [], "extra": [],
+                        "version_ok": True, "peer_version": "1.0"}
+
+        first = tool_exec.build_tool_backend(_Client(True))
+        second = tool_exec.build_tool_backend(_Client(False))
+        self.assertTrue(
+            tool_exec.wait_for_startup_tool_check(first, timeout=5)["ok"])
+        self.assertFalse(
+            tool_exec.wait_for_startup_tool_check(second, timeout=5)["ok"])
+        # 后装配的客户端不覆盖先装配的结果
+        self.assertTrue(tool_exec.startup_tool_check(first)["ok"])
+        self.assertFalse(tool_exec.startup_tool_check(second)["ok"])
+
+    def test_no_client_has_no_holder(self):
+        from agent.runtime import tool_exec
+
+        backend = tool_exec.build_tool_backend()
+        self.assertIsNone(tool_exec.startup_tool_check(backend))
+        self.assertIsNone(
+            tool_exec.wait_for_startup_tool_check(backend, timeout=0))
+
+    def test_no_module_level_mutable_slot(self):
+        from agent.runtime import tool_exec
+
+        self.assertFalse(hasattr(tool_exec, "_STARTUP_TOOL_CHECK"))
+        self.assertFalse(hasattr(tool_exec, "_STARTUP_TOOL_CHECK_THREAD"))
+
+    def test_verify_declared_tools_survives_remote_names_error(self):
+        from agent.runtime import tool_exec
+
+        class _Client:
+            def verify_tools(self, declared):
+                return {"ok": True}
+
+        original = mcp_tools.remote_tool_names
+        mcp_tools.remote_tool_names = lambda: (_ for _ in ()).throw(
+            RuntimeError("远端工具表不可用"))
+        try:
+            out = tool_exec.verify_declared_tools(_Client())
+        finally:
+            mcp_tools.remote_tool_names = original
+        self.assertFalse(out["ok"])
+        self.assertIn("reason", out)
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""长任务远端探针（agent/runtime/long_task.py::RemoteTaskProbe）单元测试。
+
+全部用**假客户端**：不连网、不起远端服务、不触模型。覆盖：
+  * 用 `hadoop_task_id` 调远端 `get_task_status`；
+  * 成功终态再取 `get_task_result`；
+  * 与 `DriverTaskProbe` 同形状（status / stage / 进度 / 阶段计数 / result）；
+  * 缺 `hadoop_task_id` 或信封失败时跳过（返回 None）。
+
+另附「登记只认任务创建类工具」的回归：`wait_for_task`（长耗时但纯等待）不登记，
+`start_cleaning_task` 登记。
+"""
+import os
+import sys
+import tempfile
+import unittest
+
+AGENT_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+REPO_ROOT = os.path.dirname(AGENT_DIR)
+sys.path.insert(0, REPO_ROOT)
+
+from agent.runtime import Runtime  # noqa: E402
+from agent.runtime.long_task import RemoteTaskProbe  # noqa: E402
+from agent.runtime.tool_exec import LongTaskBackend  # noqa: E402
+from agent.tests.stage3_helpers import FakeToolBackend  # noqa: E402
+
+
+class FakeClient(object):
+    """假远端客户端：按预置状态回 `get_task_status` / `get_task_result`，记录调用。"""
+
+    def __init__(self, status, result=None, status_envelope=None):
+        self._status = status
+        self._result = result if result is not None else {"scores": {}, "counts": {}}
+        self._status_envelope = status_envelope
+        self.calls = []
+
+    def call_tool(self, name, args):
+        self.calls.append((name, args))
+        if name == "get_task_status":
+            if self._status_envelope is not None:
+                return self._status_envelope
+            return {"ok": True, "task_id": args["task_id"], "status": self._status}
+        if name == "get_task_result":
+            envelope = {"ok": True, "task_id": args["task_id"]}
+            envelope.update(self._result)
+            return envelope
+        return {"ok": False, "error": {"code": "UNKNOWN"}}
+
+
+def test_probe_uses_hadoop_task_id():
+    client = FakeClient("running")
+    probe = RemoteTaskProbe(client)
+    out = probe.probe({"hadoop_task_id": "H1", "agent_task_id": "LT-0001"})
+    assert out["status"] == "running"
+    assert client.calls[0] == ("get_task_status", {"task_id": "H1"})
+    assert ("get_task_result", {"task_id": "H1"}) not in client.calls
+
+
+def test_probe_fetches_result_on_success():
+    client = FakeClient("succeeded", result={"counts": {"output_rows": 92}})
+    probe = RemoteTaskProbe(client)
+    out = probe.probe({"hadoop_task_id": "H1", "agent_task_id": "LT-0001"})
+    assert ("get_task_result", {"task_id": "H1"}) in client.calls
+    assert out["result"]["counts"]["output_rows"] == 92
+
+
+def test_probe_mirrors_driver_shape():
+    envelope = {"ok": True, "task_id": "H1", "status": "running",
+                "stage": "清洗", "progress_percent": 40,
+                "stage_index": 2, "stage_total": 9,
+                "message": "进行中", "errors": []}
+    probe = RemoteTaskProbe(FakeClient("running", status_envelope=envelope))
+    out = probe.probe({"hadoop_task_id": "H1"})
+    for key in ("status", "stage", "progress_percent", "stage_index",
+                "stage_total", "message", "errors"):
+        assert key in out, key
+    assert out["progress_percent"] == 40
+    assert out["stage_index"] == 2 and out["stage_total"] == 9
+
+
+def test_probe_skips_without_hadoop_id():
+    client = FakeClient("running")
+    probe = RemoteTaskProbe(client)
+    assert probe.probe({"agent_task_id": "LT-0001"}) is None
+    assert client.calls == []
+
+
+def test_probe_returns_none_on_failed_envelope():
+    probe = RemoteTaskProbe(FakeClient(
+        "running", status_envelope={"ok": False, "error": {"code": "X"}}))
+    assert probe.probe({"hadoop_task_id": "H1"}) is None
+
+
+class TestRegistrationOnlyForTaskCreators(unittest.TestCase):
+    """登记修复：只对「任务创建类」工具登记长任务，纯等待不登记。"""
+
+    def setUp(self):
+        self.tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="remote_probe_")
+        self.rt = Runtime(db_path=os.path.join(self.tmp, "rt.sqlite"),
+                          artifact_dir=os.path.join(self.tmp, "art"))
+        self.addCleanup(self._close)
+        self.cid = self.rt.create_conversation(title="探针登记测试")
+
+    def _close(self):
+        try:
+            self.rt.close()
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+
+    def test_wait_for_task_does_not_register(self):
+        inner = FakeToolBackend({"wait_for_task": {
+            "ok": True, "task_id": "H1", "status": "succeeded"}})
+        backend = LongTaskBackend(inner, self.rt.long_tasks, self.cid)
+        ok, env = backend.execute("wait_for_task", {"task_id": "H1"})
+        self.assertTrue(ok)
+        self.assertNotIn("long_task", env)
+        self.assertEqual([], self.rt.long_tasks.tasks_of(self.cid))
+
+    def test_start_cleaning_task_registers(self):
+        inner = FakeToolBackend({"start_cleaning_task": {
+            "ok": True, "task_id": "H1", "status": "queued"}})
+        backend = LongTaskBackend(inner, self.rt.long_tasks, self.cid)
+        ok, env = backend.execute("start_cleaning_task", {})
+        self.assertTrue(env.get("long_task"))
+        self.assertEqual(1, len(self.rt.long_tasks.tasks_of(self.cid)))

+class FakeMcpClient(object):
+    """假远端客户端：按工具名回罐头信封，并记下每次调用的参数。"""
+
+    def __init__(self, responses=None):
+        self.responses = dict(responses or {})
+        self.calls = []
+
+    def call_tool(self, name, args):
+        self.calls.append((name, dict(args or {})))
+        handler = self.responses.get(name)
+        if handler is None:
+            return {"ok": False, "error": {
+                "code": "MCP_UNAVAILABLE",
+                "message": "本测试没有为 %s 准备罐头信封" % name}}
+        return handler(args or {}) if callable(handler) else handler

+                         "content-length": "9"})
+

+        config.transport = httpx.MockTransport(handler)   # 仅测试：不连网
+        self.mcp.config = config
+

     def test_source_metadata_is_tool_level(self):
         """「来源」是工具级元数据；与 score_task 的评分来源参数同名但不同层。"""
         for name, spec in TOOL_SPECS.items():
             with self.subTest(tool=name):
                 # 工具级 source 是 parameters 的兄弟键，取值为 local/mcp
                 self.assertIn(spec["source"], (SOURCE_LOCAL, SOURCE_MCP))
                 self.assertIn("parameters", spec)

         scored = TOOL_SPECS["score_task"]["parameters"]["properties"]["source"]
         self.assertEqual(["raw", "published", "task"], scored["enum"])

+class TestRuntimeInjectsMcpClient(McpToolMixin, unittest.TestCase):
+    """`Runtime(mcp_client=…)` 把注入的客户端交给 source=mcp 工具；不传则诚实报错。"""
+
+    def setUp(self):
+        super(TestRuntimeInjectsMcpClient, self).setUp()
+        self.tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="stage8_mcp_inject_")
+        self.provider_calls = {"n": 0}
+
+        def forbidden_factory():
+            self.provider_calls["n"] += 1
+            raise AssertionError("离线直调不应触达模型层")
+
+        self._provider_factory = forbidden_factory
+
+    def _runtime(self, **kwargs):
+        rt = Runtime(db_path=os.path.join(self.tmp, "rt.sqlite"),
+                     artifact_dir=os.path.join(self.tmp, "art"),
+                     provider_factory=self._provider_factory, **kwargs)
+        self.addCleanup(rt.close)
+        return rt
+
+    def test_injected_client_serves_mcp_tools(self):
+        fake = FakeMcpClient(responses={self.MCP_TOOL: {
+            "ok": True, "task_id": "hadoop-99", "note": "注入客户端的结果"}})
+        rt = self._runtime(mcp_client=fake)
+        self.assertIsInstance(rt._tool_backend, RoutingBackend)
+        self.assertIs(rt._tool_backend.mcp_backend.client, fake)
+        ok, envelope = rt._tool_backend.execute(self.MCP_TOOL, {"text": "go"})
+        self.assertTrue(ok)
+        self.assertEqual(envelope["task_id"], "hadoop-99")
+        self.assertEqual(fake.calls, [(self.MCP_TOOL, {"text": "go"})])
+        self.assertEqual(self.provider_calls["n"], 0)      # 全程不触模型
+
+    def test_no_client_falls_back_to_null_and_reports_unavailable(self):
+        rt = self._runtime()                                # 不注入客户端
+        self.assertIsInstance(rt._tool_backend.mcp_backend.client,
+                              mcp_contract.NullMcpClient)
+        ok, envelope = rt._tool_backend.execute(self.MCP_TOOL, {})
+        self.assertFalse(ok)
+        self.assertEqual(envelope["error"]["code"], "MCP_UNAVAILABLE")
+
+    def test_injected_task_probe_still_works(self):
+        seen = {"status": "running", "progress_percent": 10}
+
+        class Probe(object):
+            def probe(self, record):
+                return dict(seen)
+
+        rt = self._runtime(task_probe=Probe())
+        self.assertIsNotNone(rt.long_tasks.poller._probe)
+        self.assertEqual(rt.long_tasks.poller._probe.probe({}), seen)
+

+class TestProductionRuntimeWiring(unittest.TestCase):
+    """`http_api._get_runtime` 生产装配：按配置建真实客户端 / 缺省诚实不可用。"""
+
+    def setUp(self):
+        self.tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="stage8_mcp_wiring_")
+        self._saved_runtime = http_api._RUNTIME
+        self._saved_db = runtime_module.DEFAULT_DB_PATH
+        self._saved_art = runtime_module.DEFAULT_ARTIFACT_DIR
+        runtime_module.DEFAULT_DB_PATH = os.path.join(self.tmp, "prod.sqlite")
+        runtime_module.DEFAULT_ARTIFACT_DIR = os.path.join(self.tmp, "art")
+
+        def restore():
+            http_api._RUNTIME = self._saved_runtime
+            runtime_module.DEFAULT_DB_PATH = self._saved_db
+            runtime_module.DEFAULT_ARTIFACT_DIR = self._saved_art
+
+        self.addCleanup(restore)
+
+    def _build(self):
+        http_api._RUNTIME = None
+        rt = http_api._get_runtime()
+        self.addCleanup(rt.close)
+        return rt
+
+    def test_without_config_still_starts_with_null_client(self):
+        env = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items()
+               if k not in ("AGENT_MCP_URL", "AGENT_MCP_TOKEN")}
+        with mock.patch.dict(os.environ, env, clear=True):
+            rt = self._build()
+        client = rt._tool_backend.mcp_backend.client
+        self.assertIsInstance(client, mcp_contract.NullMcpClient)
+        self.assertIsInstance(rt.long_tasks.poller._probe, RemoteTaskProbe)
+        ok, envelope = rt._tool_backend.execute("get_samples", {"task_id": "H1"})

+

+                                                 "extra": [], "version_ok": True,
+                                                 "peer_version": "1.0"}):

+  * `tools.run_and_wait` 的等待终态含 aborted：一遇中止立即返回，不再空转到超时。
+"""
+import os
+import sys
+
+AGENT_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+REPO_ROOT = os.path.dirname(AGENT_DIR)
+sys.path.insert(0, REPO_ROOT)
+
+from agent import explain  # noqa: E402
+from agent import tools  # noqa: E402
+
+
+def test_explain_maps_aborted():
+    assert explain.status_label("aborted") == "已中止"
+
+
+def test_explain_status_text_shows_aborted():
+    text = explain.explain_status({"ok": True, "task_id": "T1",
+                                   "status": "aborted"})
+    assert "已中止" in text
+
+
+def test_run_and_wait_stops_on_aborted(monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setattr(tools, "start_cleaning_task",
+                        lambda **kw: {"ok": True, "task_id": "T1"})
+    calls = []
+
+    def fake_status(tid):
+        calls.append(tid)
+        return {"ok": True, "task_id": tid, "status": "aborted"}
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(tools, "get_task_status", fake_status)
+    out = tools.run_and_wait(poll_seconds=0.001, timeout_seconds=0.2,
+                             scope="sample")
+    assert out.get("status") == "aborted"
+    assert calls == ["T1"]

new file mode 100644
index 0000000..9d8557b

+++ b/agent/tests/test_version_blocks.py

+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""结果板块：版本与时间边界（接口文档 §9.2）——有则透传、无则不给（绝不编造）。
+
+覆盖两条结果构造路：
+  * 长任务结果构造（`long_task.result_payload`）：终态时把远端信封里的
+    `versions` / `version_hashes` / `versions_source` / `time_boundaries` 原样放进板块；
+  * 离线直调结果构造（`offline.result_payload`）：走同一映射，同样透传。
+
+铁律：字段缺席时**不造空壳**（既不给 null，也不给空 dict）。
+"""
+import os
+import sys
+import unittest
+
+AGENT_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+REPO_ROOT = os.path.dirname(AGENT_DIR)
+sys.path.insert(0, REPO_ROOT)
+
+from agent.runtime import long_task, offline  # noqa: E402
+
+
+VERSIONS = {"data": "ml1m-clean-v1", "rule": "rules-v2",
+            "scoring": "score-v3", "policy": "policy-v1"}
+HASHES = {"rule": "a" * 64, "scoring": "b" * 64, "policy": "c" * 64}
+BOUNDARIES = {"T1": "2001-12-31T23:59:59Z", "T2": "2002-06-30T23:59:59Z"}
+
+
+def _record(status="succeeded"):
+    return {"agent_task_id": "LT-0001", "hadoop_task_id": "H-1",
+            "name": "数据清洗", "summary": "数据清洗", "status": status,
+            "stage": "成功", "progress_percent": 100,
+            "estimated_seconds": 30, "updated_at": 1.0}
+
+
+class TestLongTaskVersionBlocks(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_versions_and_boundaries_passed_through(self):
+        """信封带版本与时间边界 → 板块原样透传（含出处）。"""
+        result = {"versions": VERSIONS, "version_hashes": HASHES,
+                  "versions_source": "metadata", "time_boundaries": BOUNDARIES}
+        blocks = long_task.result_payload(_record(), result)["blocks"]
+        self.assertEqual(VERSIONS, blocks["versions"])
+        self.assertEqual(BOUNDARIES, blocks["time_boundaries"])
+        self.assertEqual("metadata", blocks["versions_source"])
+
+    def test_absent_versions_omitted_not_fabricated(self):
+        """信封没有这些字段 → 板块里就不出现，不拿 null / 空字典顶替。"""
+        blocks = long_task.result_payload(
+            _record(), {"counts": {"input_rows": 1}})["blocks"]
+        self.assertNotIn("versions", blocks)
+        self.assertNotIn("time_boundaries", blocks)
+        self.assertNotIn("versions_source", blocks)
+
+    def test_existing_block_keys_unchanged(self):
+        """加性改动：既有板块键一个不少。"""
+        result = {"scores": {"after": {"Accurate": 0.9}},
+                  "counts": {"input_rows": 1}, "versions": VERSIONS,
+                  "time_boundaries": BOUNDARIES}
+        blocks = long_task.result_payload(_record(), result)["blocks"]
+        for key in ("task_status", "score_comparison", "data_volume"):
+            self.assertIn(key, blocks)
+
+
+class TestOfflineVersionBlocks(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_offline_payload_passes_versions_through(self):
+        """离线直调信封同样透传版本与时间边界（走同一映射）。"""
+        envelope = {"ok": True, "task_id": "H-1", "status": "succeeded",
+                    "versions": VERSIONS, "version_hashes": HASHES,
+                    "versions_source": "result", "time_boundaries": BOUNDARIES}
+        blocks = offline.result_payload(
+            "c1", "get_task_result", {"task_id": "H-1"}, envelope)["blocks"]
+        self.assertEqual(VERSIONS, blocks["versions"])
+        self.assertEqual(BOUNDARIES, blocks["time_boundaries"])
+        self.assertEqual("result", blocks["versions_source"])
+
+    def test_offline_absent_versions_omitted(self):
+        """离线直调信封没有这些字段 → 板块里不出现。"""
+        envelope = {"ok": True, "task_id": "H-1", "status": "succeeded"}
+        blocks = offline.result_payload(
+            "c1", "get_task_result", {"task_id": "H-1"}, envelope)["blocks"]
+        self.assertNotIn("versions", blocks)
+        self.assertNotIn("time_boundaries", blocks)
+        self.assertNotIn("versions_source", blocks)
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

     last = started
     while time.time() < deadline:

         if not dc.is_ok(last):
             return last
         if callable(on_progress):
             on_progress(last)
         state = last.get("status")
-        if state in ("succeeded", "failed"):
+        if state in ("succeeded", "failed", "aborted"):
             return last
         time.sleep(poll_seconds)
 
     return {"ok": False, "error": {
         "code": dc.CODE_TIMEOUT,
         "message": "等待任务超时（%.0f 秒），任务仍在后台运行" % timeout_seconds,
         "task_id": task_id,
     }}

+// FE-9 · 收口：把早前评审里点到、但组件级用例尚未覆盖的几处契约补齐。
+// 只补真实缺口，不重复既有断言：
+//   - 进行中提示只对「排队 / 运行」出现，终态（含成功 / 失败）一律不出现；
+//   - 已中止任务的进度停在中断处（事件流既不重置、也不补到 100%）；
+//   - 产物类别辅助函数的徽标配色 / 日志识别 / 路径归类（此前只有组件级间接覆盖）。
+// `RESULT_BLOCK_META` 六块与下载地址指向 Agent 的断言已分别在 results.test.ts /
+// resources.test.ts 覆盖，此处不再重复。
+import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest'
+import { mount } from '@vue/test-utils'
+import { createPinia, setActivePinia } from 'pinia'
+import {
+  abortedHint,
+  activeHint,
+} from '@/components/progress/taskDisplay'
+import {
+  categoryTagType,
+  deriveCategoryFromPath,
+  isLogArtifact,
+} from '@/components/results/artifactCategory'
+import TaskListItem from '@/components/progress/TaskListItem.vue'
+import { useTasksStore } from '@/stores/tasks'
+import type { TaskStatus, TaskView } from '@/api/types'
+
+function task(over: Partial<TaskView> = {}): TaskView {
+  return {
+    agent_task_id: 'x1',
+    hadoop_task_id: null,
+    name: '任务',
+    summary: '',
+    status: 'running',
+    status_cn: '运行',
+    stage: '',
+    progress_percent: 0,
+    estimated_seconds: null,
+    updated_at: 0,
+    ...over,
+  }
+}
+
+describe('FE-9 · 进行中提示只在非终态出现', () => {
+  it('activeHint 仅对排队/运行给提示；abortedHint 仅对已中止给提示，其余状态均为 null', () => {
+    expect(activeHint('queued')).toContain('仍在运行')
+    expect(activeHint('running')).toContain('仍在运行')
+    for (const s of ['succeeded', 'failed', 'aborted'] as TaskStatus[]) {
+      expect(activeHint(s)).toBeNull()
+    }
+
+    const note = abortedHint('aborted')
+    expect(note).toContain('已中止')
+    expect(note).toContain('中断处')
+    for (const s of ['queued', 'running', 'succeeded', 'failed'] as TaskStatus[]) {
+      expect(abortedHint(s)).toBeNull()
+    }
+  })
+
+  it('成功/失败任务的列表项不渲染任何进行中或中止提示', () => {
+    for (const status of ['succeeded', 'failed'] as TaskStatus[]) {
+      const wrapper = mount(TaskListItem, {
+        props: { task: task({ status, progress_percent: 100 }) },
+      })
+      expect(
+        wrapper.find('[data-testid="task-running-hint-x1"]').exists(),
+      ).toBe(false)
+      expect(
+        wrapper.find('[data-testid="task-aborted-hint-x1"]').exists(),
+      ).toBe(false)
+    }
+  })
+})
+
+describe('FE-9 · 已中止进度停在中断处（事件流不重置、不补到 100%）', () => {
+  beforeEach(() => setActivePinia(createPinia()))
+
+  it('运行到 40% 后收到省略进度的中止事件，进度仍为 40%', () => {
+    const tasks = useTasksStore()
+    tasks.applyStatus(task({ progress_percent: 40, stage: '清洗' }))
+    expect(tasks.get('x1')?.progress_percent).toBe(40)
+
+    // 中止事件照远端语义可能不带 progress_percent（键缺失）；已显示的进度应原样保留。
+    tasks.applyStatus({
+      agent_task_id: 'x1',
+      hadoop_task_id: null,
+      name: '任务',
+      summary: '',
+      status: 'aborted',
+      status_cn: '已中止',
+      stage: '评分',
+      estimated_seconds: null,
+      updated_at: 0,
+    } as unknown as TaskView)
+
+    const t = tasks.get('x1')
+    expect(t?.status).toBe('aborted')
+    expect(t?.progress_percent).toBe(40)
+    expect(t?.progress_percent).not.toBe(100)
+  })
+})
+
+describe('FE-9 · 产物类别的展示契约（徽标配色 / 日志识别 / 路径归类）', () => {
+  it('类别徽标配色固定，未知/缺失类别回落 default', () => {
+    expect(categoryTagType('数据')).toBe('info')
+    expect(categoryTagType('隔离')).toBe('error')
+    expect(categoryTagType('报告')).toBe('success')
+    expect(categoryTagType('指标')).toBe('warning')
+    expect(categoryTagType('元数据')).toBe('warning')
+    expect(categoryTagType('日志')).toBe('default')
+    expect(categoryTagType('其它')).toBe('default')
+    expect(categoryTagType(undefined)).toBe('default')
+    expect(categoryTagType('没见过的类别')).toBe('default')
+  })
+
+  it('日志识别覆盖类别、logs/ 路径与 .log 文件名；路径归类粗分各类别', () => {
+    expect(isLogArtifact({ category: '日志' })).toBe(true)
+    expect(isLogArtifact({ path: 'a/logs/run.txt' })).toBe(true)
+    expect(isLogArtifact({ path: 'out/driver.log' })).toBe(true)
+    expect(isLogArtifact({ title: 'driver.log' })).toBe(true)
+    expect(isLogArtifact({ category: '报告', path: 'report.md' })).toBe(false)
+
+    expect(deriveCategoryFromPath('logs/driver.log')).toBe('日志')
+    expect(deriveCategoryFromPath('quarantine/R2.dat')).toBe('隔离')
+    expect(deriveCategoryFromPath('out/report.md')).toBe('报告')
+    expect(deriveCategoryFromPath('metrics/scores.json')).toBe('指标')
+    expect(deriveCategoryFromPath('meta/schema.json')).toBe('元数据')
+    expect(deriveCategoryFromPath('cleaned/ratings.dat')).toBe('其它')
+    expect(deriveCategoryFromPath('')).toBe('其它')
+  })
+})

+import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest'
+import { mount, type VueWrapper } from '@vue/test-utils'
+import { createPinia, setActivePinia, type Pinia } from 'pinia'
+import TaskDetail from '@/components/progress/TaskDetail.vue'
+import { useTasksStore } from '@/stores/tasks'
+import type { ResultPayload, TaskView } from '@/api/types'
+
+function task(): TaskView {
+  return {
+    agent_task_id: 'a1',
+    hadoop_task_id: 'H-1',
+    name: '数据清洗',
+    summary: '',
+    status: 'succeeded',
+    status_cn: '成功',
+    stage: '成功',
+    progress_percent: 100,
+    estimated_seconds: null,
+    updated_at: 0,
+  }
+}
+
+/** 置好 pinia 并把结果载荷塞入 tasks store（detail 经 getResult 读取）。 */
+function prime(blocks: ResultPayload['blocks']): Pinia {
+  const pinia = createPinia()
+  setActivePinia(pinia)
+  const t = task()
+  useTasksStore().applyResultPayload({ ok: true, task: t, blocks })
+  return pinia
+}
+
+function mountDetail(pinia: Pinia): VueWrapper {
+  return mount(TaskDetail, {
+    props: { task: task(), conversationId: 'c1' },
+    global: { plugins: [pinia] },
+  }) as VueWrapper
+}
+
+describe('FE-5 · 结果详情：版本与时间边界展示', () => {
+  it('有版本与时间边界时逐项显示后端原值', () => {
+    const pinia = prime({
+      report: '# 报告',
+      versions: {
+        data: 'ml1m-clean-v1',
+        rule: '1.0.0',
+        scoring: '1.0.0',
+        policy: 'policy-v1',
+      },
+      time_boundaries: {
+        T1: '2001-12-31T23:59:59Z',
+        T2: '2002-06-30T23:59:59Z',
+      },
+    })
+    const wrapper = mountDetail(pinia)
+
+    expect(wrapper.find('[data-testid="task-result-versions"]').exists()).toBe(true)
+    expect(wrapper.text()).toContain('版本')
+    expect(wrapper.find('[data-testid="task-version-data"]').text()).toContain(
+      'ml1m-clean-v1',
+    )
+    expect(wrapper.find('[data-testid="task-version-rule"]').text()).toContain(
+      '1.0.0',
+    )
+    expect(wrapper.find('[data-testid="task-version-scoring"]').text()).toContain(
+      '1.0.0',
+    )
+    expect(wrapper.find('[data-testid="task-version-policy"]').text()).toContain(
+      'policy-v1',
+    )
+
+    expect(wrapper.text()).toContain('时间边界（训练期/验证期）')
+    expect(
+      wrapper.find('[data-testid="task-time-boundary-T1"]').text(),
+    ).toContain('2001-12-31T23:59:59Z')
+    expect(
+      wrapper.find('[data-testid="task-time-boundary-T2"]').text(),
+    ).toContain('2002-06-30T23:59:59Z')
+  })
+
+  it('只给部分版本时，只显示后端给的那些键', () => {
+    const pinia = prime({
+      versions: { data: 'ml1m-clean-v1', rule: '1.0.0' },
+    })
+    const wrapper = mountDetail(pinia)
+
+    expect(wrapper.find('[data-testid="task-version-data"]').exists()).toBe(true)
+    expect(wrapper.find('[data-testid="task-version-rule"]').exists()).toBe(true)
+    expect(wrapper.find('[data-testid="task-version-scoring"]').exists()).toBe(false)
+    expect(wrapper.find('[data-testid="task-version-policy"]').exists()).toBe(false)
+  })
+
+  it('板块缺席时不显示版本/时间边界区', () => {
+    const pinia = prime({ report: '# 报告' })
+    const wrapper = mountDetail(pinia)
+
+    expect(wrapper.find('[data-testid="task-result-versions"]').exists()).toBe(false)
+    expect(wrapper.text()).not.toContain('时间边界')
+  })
+})

 /** 把错误码翻成中文；未知码给可读兜底（保留原码便于排查）。 */
 export function errorCodeText(code: string | null | undefined): string {
   if (!code) return '未知错误'
   const hit = ERROR_CODE_TEXT[code]
   if (hit) return hit
   if (code.startsWith('HTTP_')) return `请求失败（HTTP ${code.slice(5)}）`
   return `未知错误（${code}）`
 }

     }, effectiveTimeout)
   }
 
   try {
     return await fetch(url, { ...init, signal: controller.signal })
   } catch (err) {
     if (timedOut) {
       throw new ApiError('TIMEOUT', errorCodeText('TIMEOUT'), { cause: err })
     }
     if (controller.signal.aborted) {
-      throw new ApiError('ABORTED', errorCodeText('ABORTED'), { cause: err })
+      throw new ApiError(
+        'REQUEST_CANCELLED',
+        errorCodeText('REQUEST_CANCELLED'),
+        { cause: err },
+      )
     }
     throw new ApiError('NETWORK_ERROR', errorCodeText('NETWORK_ERROR'), {
       cause: err,
     })
   } finally {
     if (timer) clearTimeout(timer)
     if (externalSignal) externalSignal.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort)
   }
 }

+/** 结果六块的顺序与文案。 */
 export const RESULT_BLOCK_META: readonly ResultBlockMeta[] = [
-  { key: 'score_comparison', label: '五维评分对比', tab: 'score' },
-  { key: 'data_volume', label: '数据量变化', tab: 'evidence' },

+  { key: 'score_comparison', label: '五维评分对比', artifactCategory: '评分' },
+  { key: 'data_volume', label: '数据量变化', artifactCategory: '证据' },
+  { key: 'quarantine_samples', label: '隔离样例', artifactCategory: '证据' },
+  { key: 'report', label: '评估报告', artifactCategory: '报告' },

+  { key: 'task_status', label: '任务状态', artifactCategory: null },
 ]
 
-/** 从结果块集合中挑出「有值」的可跳转块。 */
+/** 从结果块集合中挑出「有值」的块。 */
 export function presentResultBlocks(
   blocks: ResultBlocks | null | undefined,
 ): ResultBlockMeta[] {
   if (!blocks) return []
   return RESULT_BLOCK_META.filter(
     (meta) => blocks[meta.key] !== undefined && blocks[meta.key] !== null,
   )
 }
+
+/**
+ * 在产物列表里找该块对应的产物（按 `artifactCategory` 取第一个匹配项）。
+ * 该块无独立产物、或列表里没有同类产物时返回 `null`（调用方据此只展示、不导航）。
+ */
+export function artifactForBlock(
+  meta: ResultBlockMeta,
+  artifacts: readonly ArtifactRef[] | null | undefined,
+): ArtifactRef | null {
+  if (!meta.artifactCategory) return null
+  return (
+    (artifacts ?? []).find((a) => a.category === meta.artifactCategory) ?? null
+  )
+}

 /** 状态配色。 */
 export function statusTagType(status: TaskStatus): TaskTagType {
   return STATUS_TAG_TYPES[status] ?? 'default'
 }
 
 /** 是否终态。 */
 export function isTerminal(status: TaskStatus): boolean {
   return TERMINAL_STATUSES.has(status)
 }
 
+/** 非终态的进行中状态（排队 / 运行）：任务仍在跑，进度还会继续。 */
+export function isActiveTask(status: TaskStatus): boolean {
+  return status === 'queued' || status === 'running'
+}
+
+/** 进行中的提示文案；非进行中返回 null（不展示）。 */
+export function activeHint(status: TaskStatus): string | null {
+  return isActiveTask(status) ? '仍在运行，可继续等待' : null
+}
+
+/** 已中止的提示文案；非中止返回 null（不展示）。进度停在中断处、不强制到 100%。 */
+export function abortedHint(status: TaskStatus): string | null {
+  return status === 'aborted' ? '任务已中止，进度停在中断处' : null
+}
+
 /** 是否给出预计时长；无（null/undefined/非正）则如实不显示。 */
 export function hasEstimate(task: Pick<TaskView, 'estimated_seconds'>): boolean {
   const seconds = task.estimated_seconds
   return typeof seconds === 'number' && Number.isFinite(seconds) && seconds > 0
 }
 
 /** 进度百分比收敛到 [0, 100] 的整数。 */
 export function clampPercent(value: number | null | undefined): number {
   if (typeof value !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(value)) return 0
   return Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Math.round(value)))

+// 产物类别（§3.12）的展示映射与判定：徽标配色、日志类识别、按路径归类。
+// 纯函数、无依赖，供产物标签（ArtifactView）与产出文件区（TaskFilesSection）共用。
+import type { ArtifactCategory } from '@/api/types'
+
+/** Naive UI `n-tag` 支持的配色。 */
+export type CategoryTagType =
+  | 'default'
+  | 'info'
+  | 'success'
+  | 'warning'
+  | 'error'
+
+/** 类别 → 徽标配色：数据=info、报告=success、日志=default、指标/元数据=warning、隔离=error、其它=default。 */
+const CATEGORY_TAG_TYPE: Record<ArtifactCategory, CategoryTagType> = {
+  数据: 'info',
+  隔离: 'error',
+  报告: 'success',
+  指标: 'warning',
+  元数据: 'warning',
+  日志: 'default',
+  其它: 'default',
+}
+
+/** 未知/缺失类别一律按 `default`，保证「category 宽松接收」也有稳定配色。 */
+export function categoryTagType(category: string | null | undefined): CategoryTagType {
+  if (!category) return 'default'
+  return CATEGORY_TAG_TYPE[category as ArtifactCategory] ?? 'default'
+}
+
+/** basename 是否 `driver.log` 这类日志文件名（以 `.log` 结尾）。 */
+export function isLogFileName(path: string | null | undefined): boolean {
+  const name = String(path ?? '').split(/[\\/]/).pop() ?? ''
+  return /\.log$/i.test(name)
+}
+
+/** 路径是否落在 `logs/` 段下（`logs/x`、`a/logs/x` 都算）。 */
+export function isLogPath(path: string | null | undefined): boolean {
+  const p = String(path ?? '')
+  if (!p) return false
+  return /(^|[\\/])logs[\\/]/i.test(p)
+}
+
+/** 产物是否为日志类：类别为「日志」、路径在 `logs/` 下、或文件名形如 `driver.log`。 */
+export function isLogArtifact(artifact: {
+  category?: string | null
+  path?: string | null
+  title?: string | null
+}): boolean {
+  if (artifact.category === '日志') return true
+  if (isLogPath(artifact.path)) return true
+  return isLogFileName(artifact.path) || isLogFileName(artifact.title)
+}
+
+/** 按路径粗分产物类别（产出文件行只给 path/kind，没有远端 category）。 */
+export function deriveCategoryFromPath(
+  path: string | null | undefined,
+): ArtifactCategory {
+  const p = String(path ?? '').toLowerCase()
+  if (!p) return '其它'
+  if (isLogPath(p) || isLogFileName(p)) return '日志'
+  if (p.includes('quarantine') || p.includes('isolat')) return '隔离'
+  if (p.includes('report')) return '报告'
+  if (p.includes('metric') || p.includes('score')) return '指标'
+  if (p.includes('meta') || p.includes('schema')) return '元数据'
+  return '其它'
+}

+/** 四类版本的展示标签；出现未知键时回落为该键原样。 */
+const VERSION_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
+  data: '数据版本',
+  rule: '规则版本',
+  scoring: '评分方案版本',
+  policy: '策略版本',

+/** 两个时间边界的展示标签；出现未知键时回落为该键原样。 */
+const TIME_BOUNDARY_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
+  T1: '训练期截止（T1）',
+  T2: '验证期截止（T2）',
+}
+
+/** 把后端值转成展示文本；字符串原样，带 version 的对象取其 version，其余 JSON。 */
+function displayText(value: unknown): string {
+  if (typeof value === 'string') return value
+  if (value === null || value === undefined) return ''
+  if (isRecord(value)) {
+    if (typeof value.version === 'string') return value.version
+    try {
+      return JSON.stringify(value)
+    } catch {
+      return String(value)
+    }
+  }
+  return String(value)
+}
+
+/** 把 `versions` 拆成展示行：后端给哪些键就显示哪些，顺序照后端。 */
+export function versionRows(raw: unknown): VersionRow[] {
+  if (!isRecord(raw)) return []
+  return Object.entries(raw).map(([key, value]) => ({
+    key,
+    label: VERSION_LABELS[key] ?? key,
+    value: displayText(value),
+  }))
+}
+
+/** 把 `time_boundaries` 拆成展示行：T1、T2 在前，其余键照后端顺序。 */
+export function timeBoundaryRows(raw: unknown): TimeBoundaryRow[] {
+  if (!isRecord(raw)) return []
+  const preferred = ['T1', 'T2'].filter((key) => key in raw)
+  const rest = Object.keys(raw).filter((key) => !preferred.includes(key))
+  return [...preferred, ...rest].map((key) => ({
+    key,
+    label: TIME_BOUNDARY_LABELS[key] ?? key,
+    value: displayText(raw[key]),
+  }))
+}
